          &quot;≤&quot;:&quot;disuguaglianza ≤&quot;,&quot;≥&quot;:&quot;disuguaglianza ≥&quot;,&quot;≠&quot;:&quot;disuguaglianza ≠&quot;,
          &quot;→&quot;:&quot;implicazione/trasformazione&quot;,&quot;⇒&quot;:&quot;implicazione/trasformazione&quot;,&quot;⟹&quot;:&quot;implicazione/trasformazione&quot;,
          &quot;⇔&quot;:&quot;equivalenza logica&quot;,&quot;⟺&quot;:&quot;equivalenza logica&quot;,&quot;↔&quot;:&quot;relazione bidirezionale&quot;,
          &quot;∼&quot;:&quot;relazione asintotica/equivalente&quot;,&quot;≜&quot;:&quot;definizione/identità&quot;,&quot;≔&quot;:&quot;definizione/identità&quot;,&quot;:=&quot;:&quot;definizione/identità&quot;
        }[eq.operator] || &quot;non esatta&quot;;
        results.push({nodeId:node.id,step:eq.step,status:&quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot;,message:`Relazione ${relationLabel} rilevata; il livello algebrico 1.3 tratta le relazioni non esatte in modo conservativo e non le assume come uguaglianze.`,expression:eq.raw});
        continue;
      }
      const rel=relationPoly(eq);
      if(!rel){
        review++;
        results.push({
          nodeId:node.id,
          step:eq.step,
          status:&quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot;,
          message:&quot;Equazione rilevata ma non normalizzabile automaticamente dal livello algebrico 1.3.&quot;,
          expression:eq.raw
        });
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * HARD NUMERIC GATE
       * -----------------
       * A relation between two polynomial forms is not, by itself, proof
       * that a numerical equality is true.  In particular, proportional
       * relation polynomials can describe two false equations.  Therefore
       * a fully numeric equation MUST be evaluated before identity or
       * transformation classification is allowed.
       *
       * Example: 7 × 8 = 54
       *   LHS = 56, RHS = 54
       *   =&gt; ALGEBRA_FAIL, never ALGEBRA_TRANSFORM / ALGEBRA_IDENTITY.
       */
      const leftAst = parseExpression(eq.left);
      const rightAst = parseExpression(eq.right);
      const leftValue = leftAst ? evalNumericAlgebraic(leftAst) : null;
      const rightValue = rightAst ? evalNumericAlgebraic(rightAst) : null;
      const numericEquation = leftValue !== null &amp;&amp; rightValue !== null;

      if(numericEquation){
        const delta = Math.abs(leftValue - rightValue);
        const scale = Math.max(1, Math.abs(leftValue), Math.abs(rightValue));
        const equal = delta &lt;= 1e-12 * scale;

        if(!equal){
          failures++;
          results.push({
            nodeId:node.id,
            step:eq.step,
            status:&quot;ALGEBRA_FAIL&quot;,
            message:`Uguaglianza numerica falsa: ${leftValue} ≠ ${rightValue}.`,
            expression:eq.raw,
            leftValue,
            rightValue
          });
          continue;
        }

        /*
         * A true numeric equality has passed the independent truth gate.
         * We deliberately do NOT stop here: the normal identity /
         * transformation classifier may still classify a true step as an
         * identity or as a valid transformation relative to the previous
         * step. The gate only blocks false numeric equations.
         */
      }

      /* Symbolic identity: only a zero normalized relation is sufficient. */
      if(rel.size===0){
        verified++;
        results.push({
          nodeId:node.id,
          step:eq.step,
          status:&quot;ALGEBRA_IDENTITY&quot;,
          message:&quot;Identità algebrica verificata: i due membri sono equivalenti.&quot;,
          expression:eq.raw
        });
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Transformation is reserved for symbolic equations whose normalized
       * relations are proportional.  Numeric false equations have already
       * been rejected by the hard gate above.
       */
      if(i&gt;0){
        const prev=relationPoly(equations[i-1]);
        if(prev&amp;&amp;proportional(rel,prev)){
          transformed++;
          results.push({
            nodeId:node.id,
            step:eq.step,
            status:&quot;ALGEBRA_TRANSFORM&quot;,
            message:&quot;Trasformazione algebrica verificata rispetto allo step precedente (equazioni equivalenti a fattore non nullo).&quot;,
            expression:eq.raw
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  /* Collapse duplicate diagnostics for the same mathematical step.
     The analysis counters remain unchanged; only the UI/log representation
     is de-duplicated so one unresolved step is reported once. */
  const diagnosticResults = [];
  const seenDiagnostics = new Set();
  for (const item of results) {
    const key = `${item.nodeId}|${item.step}|${item.status}`;
    if (item.status === &quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot;) {
      if (seenDiagnostics.has(key)) continue;
      seenDiagnostics.add(key);
    }
    diagnosticResults.push(item);
  }
  const score=checked===0?100:Math.max(0,Math.round(100-(failures/checked)*100));
  return {checked,verified,transformed,review,failures,score,grade:gradeFor(score),results:diagnosticResults,status:failures&gt;0?&quot;FAIL&quot;:review&gt;0?&quot;REVIEW&quot;:&quot;PASS&quot;};
}

/* =========================================================
   LOGICAL CONSISTENCY ANALYSIS
   =========================================================
   Conservative language-level checks. These are diagnostics, not a
   substitute for a formal logic prover or natural-language theorem prover.
*/
function analyzeLogicalProofs(data) {
  const nodes = Array.isArray(data?.nodes) ? data.nodes : [];
  const byId = new Map(nodes.filter(n =&gt; n &amp;&amp; n.id).map(n =&gt; [n.id, n]));
  const proofKinds = new Set([&quot;proposition&quot;, &quot;lemma&quot;, &quot;theorem&quot;, &quot;corollary&quot;, &quot;self_validation&quot;, &quot;meta_validation&quot;]);
  const findings = [];
  let checkedSteps = 0;

  const normalize = s =&gt; String(s || &quot;&quot;)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[“”&quot;&#39;]/g, &quot;&quot;)
    .replace(/\s+/g, &quot; &quot;)
    .trim();

  const stripPrefix = s =&gt; normalize(s)
    .replace(/^step\s*\d+\s*[:.)-]?\s*/, &quot;&quot;)
    .replace(/^(therefore|thus|hence|pertanto|quindi|dunque)\s+/i, &quot;&quot;)
    .replace(/^(assume|assuming|let us assume|assumiamo|supponiamo)\s+/i, &quot;&quot;);

  const canonicalEquation = text =&gt; {
    const m = String(text || &quot;&quot;).match(/([^;,.\n]{1,300}?)(?:=|≡|≈)([^;,.\n]{1,300})/);
    if (!m) return null;
    const norm = x =&gt; normalize(x).replace(/[×·]/g, &quot;*&quot;).replace(/[−–]/g, &quot;-&quot;);
    return { left: norm(m[1]), right: norm(m[2]), key: `${norm(m[1])} = ${norm(m[2])}` };
  };

  /*
   * v0.8.1: valutatore aritmetico scritto a mano (nessun Function()/eval),
   * coerente con il resto del motore (vedi evalNumeric / evalNumericAlgebraic).
   * Limitato a +,-,*,/,^, parentesi e numeri: nessuna identificazione di
   * variabili, quindi resta un semplice controllo di &quot;letterale numerico&quot;.
   */
  const numericLiteral = expr =&gt; {
    const x = String(expr || &quot;&quot;).replace(/,/g, &quot;.&quot;).replace(/[×·]/g, &quot;*&quot;).replace(/[÷]/g, &quot;/&quot;).replace(/[−–]/g, &quot;-&quot;).trim();
    if (!/^[0-9+\-*/().\s^]+$/.test(x) || !/[0-9]/.test(x)) return null;
    const src = x.replace(/\s+/g, &quot;&quot;);
    let i = 0;
    function peek() { return src[i]; }
    function primary() {
      if (src[i] === &quot;(&quot;) {
        i++;
        const v = addsub();
        if (v == null || src[i] !== &quot;)&quot;) return null;
        i++;
        return v;
      }
      if (src[i] === &quot;+&quot; || src[i] === &quot;-&quot;) {
        const sign = src[i]; i++;
        const v = primary();
        if (v == null) return null;
        return sign === &quot;-&quot; ? -v : v;
      }
      const start = i;
      while (i &lt; src.length &amp;&amp; /[0-9.]/.test(src[i])) i++;
      if (i === start) return null;
      const raw = src.slice(start, i);
      if ((raw.match(/\./g) || []).length &gt; 1 || raw === &quot;.&quot;) return null;
      return Number(raw);
    }
    function power() {
      const a = primary();
      if (a == null) return null;
      if (peek() === &quot;^&quot;) { i++; const b = power(); if (b == null) return null; return Math.pow(a, b); }
      return a;
    }
    function muldiv() {
      let a = power();
      if (a == null) return null;
      while (peek() === &quot;*&quot; || peek() === &quot;/&quot;) {
        const op = src[i++];
        const b = power();
        if (b == null) return null;
        a = op === &quot;*&quot; ? a * b : (b === 0 ? null : a / b);
        if (a == null) return null;
      }
      return a;
    }
    function addsub() {
      let a = muldiv();
      if (a == null) return null;
      while (peek() === &quot;+&quot; || peek() === &quot;-&quot;) {
        const op = src[i++];
        const b = muldiv();
        if (b == null) return null;
        a = op === &quot;+&quot; ? a + b : a - b;
      }
      return a;
    }
    const value = addsub();
    if (value == null || i !== src.length) return null;
    return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
  };

  const addFinding = (nodeId, step, status, message, expression) =&gt; {
    findings.push({ nodeId, step, status, message, expression });
  };

  for (const node of nodes) {
    if (!proofKinds.has(node.kind) || !node.proof || !Array.isArray(node.proof.steps)) continue;

    const steps = node.proof.steps.map((x, i) =&gt; ({
      raw: typeof x === &quot;string&quot; ? x : JSON.stringify(x),
      n: i + 1
    }));
    checkedSteps += steps.length;

    const normalized = steps.map(s =&gt; ({ ...s, text: stripPrefix(s.raw) }));
    const seen = new Map();
    const equations = [];

    normalized.forEach(s =&gt; {
      if (s.text.length &gt;= 3) {
        if (seen.has(s.text)) {
          addFinding(node.id, s.n, &quot;LOGICAL_REVIEW&quot;, &quot;Lo stesso contenuto compare in più step: possibile riutilizzo della conclusione.&quot;, s.raw);
        } else {
          seen.set(s.text, s.n);
        }
      }

      const eq = canonicalEquation(s.raw);
      if (eq) equations.push({ ...eq, step: s.n, raw: s.raw });
    });

    /*
     * v0.8.1 — corpus di testo usati dal rilevatore LOGICAL_GAP sotto:
     * nome/descrizione/proof delle dipendenze dichiarate (deps) e del
     * nodo stesso, normalizzati una sola volta per nodo.
     */
    const dependencyCorpus = normalize(
      (Array.isArray(node.deps) ? node.deps : [])
        .map(depId =&gt; {
          const dep = byId.get(depId);
          if (!dep) return &quot;&quot;;
          const depSteps = dep.proof &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(dep.proof.steps)
            ? dep.proof.steps.map(x =&gt; typeof x === &quot;string&quot; ? x : JSON.stringify(x)).join(&quot; &quot;)
            : &quot;&quot;;
          return `${dep.name || &quot;&quot;} ${dep.description || &quot;&quot;} ${depSteps}`;
        })
        .join(&quot; &quot;)
    );
    const selfCorpus = normalize(`${node.name || &quot;&quot;} ${node.description || &quot;&quot;}`);

    /*
     * v0.8.1 — LOGICAL_GAP detector (volutamente conservativo).
     * Esamina SOLO l&#39;equazione finale della dimostrazione, e solo quando
     * il suo membro destro è un simbolo isolato (es. &quot;... = k&quot;), mai
     * un&#39;espressione composta: analizzare in sicurezza le variabili
     * dentro un&#39;espressione composta richiederebbe un parser algebrico
     * completo, cosa che questo controllo di livello linguistico non
     * tenta deliberatamente di fare (per evitare falsi positivi). Se
     * quel simbolo non compare mai altrove — non in nessun altro step
     * della stessa dimostrazione, non nel nome/descrizione del nodo, non
     * nel nome/descrizione/proof delle dipendenze dichiarate (deps) —
     * la conclusione si appoggia a un termine mai introdotto né
     * giustificato in tutto l&#39;argomento.
     *
     * Definire una nuova variabile durante una derivazione (es. &quot;y = x+1&quot;)
     * NON viene mai segnalato: è normale prassi dimostrativa. Solo un
     * riferimento nella conclusione a un simbolo mai definito né
     * ereditato dalle fondamenta è trattato come possibile lacuna.
     */
    if (equations.length &gt; 0) {
      const finalEq = equations[equations.length - 1];
      const rhsSymbol = finalEq.right.trim();
      if (/^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*$/i.test(rhsSymbol)) {
        const elsewhereText = normalize(
          steps.filter(s =&gt; s.n !== finalEq.step).map(s =&gt; s.raw).join(&quot; &quot;)
        );
        const escaped = rhsSymbol.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, &quot;\\$&amp;&quot;);
        const symbolPattern = new RegExp(`(^|[^a-z0-9_])${escaped}([^a-z0-9_]|$)`, &quot;i&quot;);
        const introducedElsewhere =
          symbolPattern.test(elsewhereText) ||
          symbolPattern.test(dependencyCorpus) ||
          symbolPattern.test(selfCorpus);
        if (!introducedElsewhere) {
          addFinding(
            node.id,
            finalEq.step,
            &quot;LOGICAL_GAP&quot;,
            `La conclusione fa riferimento al simbolo &quot;${rhsSymbol}&quot; che non compare in nessun altro step della dimostrazione, nella descrizione del nodo o nelle fondamenta dichiarate (deps): possibile lacuna logica (LOGICAL_GAP; richiede revisione).`,
            finalEq.raw
          );
        }
      }
    }

    /* Explicit circular reasoning patterns. */
    const allText = normalized.map(s =&gt; s.text).join(&quot; &quot;);
    const circularPatterns = [
      /(.+)\s+(?:perché|because)\s+(.+)\s+(.+)\s+(?:perché|because)\s+\1/,
      /(.+)\s+(?:because|perché)\s+(.+?)[.!?]?\s+\2\s+(?:because|perché)\s+\1/,
      /a\s+è\s+vero\s+perché\s+b\s+è\s+vero.*b\s+è\s+vero\s+perché\s+a\s+è\s+vero/,
      /a\s+is\s+true\s+because\s+b\s+is\s+true.*b\s+is\s+true\s+because\s+a\s+is\s+true/
    ];
    if (circularPatterns.some(re =&gt; re.test(allText))) {
      addFinding(node.id, 1, &quot;LOGICAL_FAIL&quot;, &quot;Possibile ragionamento circolare: una proposizione viene giustificata attraverso una proposizione che dipende dalla prima.&quot;);
    }

    /* Detect contradictory assignments or incompatible equations inside the same proof. */
    const assignments = new Map();
    for (const eq of equations) {
      const l = eq.left.trim(), r = eq.right.trim();
      const lv = numericLiteral(l), rv = numericLiteral(r);
      if (lv == null &amp;&amp; rv != null &amp;&amp; /^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*$/i.test(l)) {
        if (!assignments.has(l)) assignments.set(l, []);
        assignments.get(l).push({ value: rv, step: eq.step });
      } else if (rv == null &amp;&amp; lv != null &amp;&amp; /^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*$/i.test(r)) {
        if (!assignments.has(r)) assignments.set(r, []);
        assignments.get(r).push({ value: lv, step: eq.step });
      }
    }
    for (const [variable, vals] of assignments.entries()) {
      const unique = [...new Set(vals.map(v =&gt; v.value))];
      if (unique.length &gt; 1) {
        for (const item of vals.slice(1)) {
          addFinding(node.id, item.step, &quot;LOGICAL_CONTRADICTION&quot;, `Contraddizione numerica: ${variable} viene associata a valori incompatibili (${unique.join(&quot; e &quot;)}).`);
        }
      }
    }

    /* Also compare identical left-hand sides such as &quot;2+3 = 5&quot; and &quot;2+3 = 6&quot;. */
    const lhsMap = new Map();
    for (const eq of equations) {
      const lv = numericLiteral(eq.left);
      const rv = numericLiteral(eq.right);
      if (lv != null &amp;&amp; rv != null) {
        const key = eq.left.replace(/\s+/g, &quot;&quot;);
        if (!lhsMap.has(key)) lhsMap.set(key, []);
        lhsMap.get(key).push({ value: rv, step: eq.step });
      }
    }
    for (const [lhs, vals] of lhsMap.entries()) {
      const unique = [...new Set(vals.map(v =&gt; v.value))];
      if (unique.length &gt; 1) {
        for (const item of vals.slice(1)) {
          addFinding(node.id, item.step, &quot;LOGICAL_CONTRADICTION&quot;, `Contraddizione numerica: la stessa espressione è stata associata a ${unique.join(&quot; e &quot;)}.`);
        }
      }
    }

    normalized.forEach((s, idx) =&gt; {
      const text = s.text;
      const hasAssumption = /^(assumiamo|supponiamo|assume|assuming|let us assume)\b/.test(text);
      const hasTherefore = /^(pertanto|quindi|dunque|therefore|thus|hence)\b/.test(text);

      if (hasAssumption &amp;&amp; idx === normalized.length - 1) {
        addFinding(node.id, s.n, &quot;LOGICAL_REVIEW&quot;, &quot;L&#39;ultimo step introduce un&#39;assunzione senza una successiva derivazione esplicita.&quot;, s.raw);
      }

      if (hasTherefore) {
        const conclusion = text.replace(/^(pertanto|quindi|dunque|therefore|thus|hence)\s+/, &quot;&quot;).trim();
        const earlier = normalized.slice(0, idx).some(x =&gt; x.text === conclusion);
        if (earlier) {
          addFinding(node.id, s.n, &quot;LOGICAL_ASSUMED&quot;, &quot;La conclusione compare già nelle premesse o negli step precedenti: possibile conclusione assunta anziché derivata.&quot;, s.raw);
        }
      }
    });

    /* Exact repeated final equation: conclusion already assumed. */
    if (equations.length &gt;= 2) {
      const lastEq = equations[equations.length - 1];
      const priorSame = equations.slice(0, -1).some(e =&gt; e.left === lastEq.left &amp;&amp; e.right === lastEq.right);
      if (priorSame) {
        addFinding(node.id, lastEq.step, &quot;LOGICAL_ASSUMED&quot;, &quot;La conclusione compare già nelle premesse o negli step precedenti: possibile conclusione assunta anziché derivata.&quot;, lastEq.raw);
      }
    }

    /* Conservative unsupported-conclusion detection for explicit final numeric claims. */
    if (equations.length &gt;= 2) {
      const lastEq = equations[equations.length - 1];
      const prior = equations.slice(0, -1);
      const rhsNumeric = numericLiteral(lastEq.right);
      const lhsVariable = /^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*$/i.test(lastEq.left.trim());
      const conclusionAlreadyPresent = prior.some(e =&gt; e.left === lastEq.left &amp;&amp; e.right === lastEq.right);
      if (rhsNumeric != null &amp;&amp; lhsVariable &amp;&amp; !conclusionAlreadyPresent) {
        const related = prior.some(e =&gt; e.left.includes(lastEq.left) || e.right.includes(lastEq.left));
        const supportsExactValue = prior.some(e =&gt; {
          const v = numericLiteral(e.right);
          return e.left === lastEq.left &amp;&amp; v != null &amp;&amp; Math.abs(v - rhsNumeric) &lt; 1e-12;
        });
        if (related &amp;&amp; !supportsExactValue) {
          addFinding(node.id, lastEq.step, &quot;LOGICAL_UNSUPPORTED&quot;, &quot;Conclusione numerica non supportata esplicitamente dagli step precedenti: possibile salto logico.&quot;, lastEq.raw);
        }
      }
    }

    /* Linguistic conclusion without an explicit mathematical transformation. */
    if (steps.length &gt;= 3) {
      const last = normalized[normalized.length - 1];
      const prior = normalized.slice(0, -1);
      const hasBareConclusion = /^(pertanto|quindi|dunque|therefore|thus|hence)\b/.test(last.text);
      if (hasBareConclusion &amp;&amp; prior.length &gt;= 2 &amp;&amp; !/[=≡≈→]/.test(last.raw)) {
        addFinding(node.id, last.n, &quot;LOGICAL_REVIEW&quot;, &quot;Conclusione linguistica senza trasformazione matematica esplicita: verificare che il passaggio sia giustificato dalle premesse.&quot;, last.raw);
      }
    }
  }

  /*
   * v0.8.1: &quot;LOGICAL_GAP&quot; è ora prodotto dal rilevatore sopra (simbolo
   * introdotto senza alcun collegamento testuale a step precedenti,
   * descrizione del nodo o dipendenze dichiarate). Resta un controllo
   * conservativo basato su testo, non una prova logica formale.
   */
  const criticalStatuses = new Set([&quot;LOGICAL_FAIL&quot;, &quot;LOGICAL_CONTRADICTION&quot;, &quot;LOGICAL_UNSUPPORTED&quot;, &quot;LOGICAL_ASSUMED&quot;, &quot;LOGICAL_GAP&quot;]);
  const criticalFindings = findings.filter(f =&gt; criticalStatuses.has(f.status));
  const reviewFindings = findings.filter(f =&gt; !criticalStatuses.has(f.status));
  const failures = criticalFindings.length;
  const warnings = reviewFindings.length;
  const score = checkedSteps === 0 ? 100 : Math.max(0, Math.round(100 - (failures / checkedSteps) * 100));

  const counts = {
    circular: findings.filter(f =&gt; f.status === &quot;LOGICAL_FAIL&quot;).length,
    contradictions: findings.filter(f =&gt; f.status === &quot;LOGICAL_CONTRADICTION&quot;).length,
    unsupported: findings.filter(f =&gt; f.status === &quot;LOGICAL_UNSUPPORTED&quot;).length,
    assumedConclusion: findings.filter(f =&gt; f.status === &quot;LOGICAL_ASSUMED&quot;).length,
    logicalGaps: findings.filter(f =&gt; f.status === &quot;LOGICAL_GAP&quot;).length
  };

  return {
    checkedSteps,
    findings,
    failures,
    warnings,
    score,
    grade: gradeFor(score),
    counts,
    status: failures ? &quot;FAIL&quot; : warnings ? &quot;REVIEW&quot; : &quot;PASS&quot;
  };
}

/* =========================================================
   FORMAL LAYER
   ========================================================= */

function validateFormal(
  data,
  structural
) {

  const checks = [];
  let passed = 0;

  function check(label, condition, code = null) {
    const ok = !!condition;
    checks.push({ label, passed: ok, code });
    if (ok) passed++;
  }

  const nodes = structural.nodes;

  check(
    &quot;Formal node array available&quot;,
    Array.isArray(nodes),
    &quot;FORM_NO_NODE_ARRAY&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;All node IDs are strings&quot;,
    nodes.every(n =&gt; typeof n.id === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; n.id.length &gt; 0),
    &quot;FORM_BAD_NODE_ID&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;All dependency arrays are valid&quot;,
    nodes.every(n =&gt; Array.isArray(n.deps)),
    &quot;FORM_BAD_DEPS_ARRAY&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;No invalid dependency edge&quot;,
    structural.edges.every(e =&gt; !e.invalid),
    &quot;FORM_INVALID_EDGE&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;No circular dependency&quot;,
    structural.cycles.length === 0,
    &quot;FORM_CIRCULAR_DEP&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;No self dependency&quot;,
    structural.errors.every(e =&gt; !e.includes(&quot;Auto-dipendenza&quot;)),
    &quot;FORM_SELF_DEP&quot;
  );

  const theoremNodes = nodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;theorem&quot;);

  check(
    &quot;Theorems have dependencies&quot;,
    theoremNodes.every(
      n =&gt; Array.isArray(n.deps) &amp;&amp; n.deps.length &gt; 0
    ),
    &quot;FORM_THEOREM_NO_DEPS&quot;
  );

  /*
   * Two legitimate proof representations are supported:
   *
   * 1) external formal proof metadata:
   *      system + statement + steps + verified
   *
   * 2) ADEC validation evidence:
   *      method + steps + status
   *      status ∈ verified | declared | pending | proposed | unverified
   *
   * The second form is NOT treated as an external mathematical proof.
   * It only proves that the theory contains a declared validation record.
   */
  function hasValidationEvidence(node) {
    const proof = node &amp;&amp; node.proof;
    return !!proof &amp;&amp;
      typeof proof === &quot;object&quot; &amp;&amp;
      typeof proof.method === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
      proof.method.trim().length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      Array.isArray(proof.steps) &amp;&amp;
      proof.steps.length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      ([&quot;verified&quot;, &quot;declared&quot;, &quot;pending&quot;, &quot;proposed&quot;, &quot;unverified&quot;].includes(proof.status));
  }

  function hasExternalFormalProof(node) {
    const proof = node &amp;&amp; node.proof;
    return !!proof &amp;&amp;
      typeof proof === &quot;object&quot; &amp;&amp;
      typeof proof.system === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
      proof.system.trim().length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      typeof proof.statement === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
      proof.statement.trim().length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      Array.isArray(proof.steps) &amp;&amp;
      proof.steps.length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      (proof.verified === true || proof.status === &quot;verified&quot;);
  }

  const proofBearingNodes = nodes.filter(n =&gt;
    [&quot;proposition&quot;, &quot;lemma&quot;, &quot;theorem&quot;, &quot;corollary&quot;, &quot;self_validation&quot;, &quot;meta_validation&quot;]
      .includes(n.kind)
  );

  const proofEvidenceNodes = proofBearingNodes.filter(hasValidationEvidence);
  const externalProofNodes = proofBearingNodes.filter(hasExternalFormalProof);

  /*
   * Proof coverage is deliberately separated from formal representation.
   * A theorem/proposition without a proof object is NOT a malformed node.
   * It simply has no proof coverage supplied in this JSON.
   */
  check(
    &quot;Supplied proof/evidence objects are structurally valid&quot;,
    proofBearingNodes.every(n =&gt; {
      if (n.proof == null) return true;
      if (!n.proof || typeof n.proof !== &quot;object&quot; || Array.isArray(n.proof)) return false;
      const hasEvidence = hasValidationEvidence(n);
      const hasExternal = hasExternalFormalProof(n);
      return hasEvidence || hasExternal;
    }),
    &quot;FORM_BAD_PROOF_OBJECT&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Supplied validation evidence has steps&quot;,
    proofBearingNodes.every(n =&gt; {
      if (n.proof == null) return true;
      if (hasExternalFormalProof(n)) return true;
      return hasValidationEvidence(n);
    }),
    &quot;FORM_PROOF_NO_STEPS&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Supplied validation evidence has method&quot;,
    proofBearingNodes.every(n =&gt; {
      if (n.proof == null) return true;
      if (hasExternalFormalProof(n)) return true;
      return typeof n.proof.method === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; n.proof.method.trim().length &gt; 0;
    }),
    &quot;FORM_PROOF_NO_METHOD&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Supplied validation evidence has status&quot;,
    proofBearingNodes.every(n =&gt; {
      if (n.proof == null) return true;
      if (hasExternalFormalProof(n)) return true;
      return [&quot;verified&quot;, &quot;declared&quot;, &quot;pending&quot;, &quot;proposed&quot;, &quot;unverified&quot;].includes(n.proof.status);
    }),
    &quot;FORM_PROOF_NO_STATUS&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;External formal proofs are internally complete when present&quot;,
    externalProofNodes.every(
      n =&gt;
        typeof n.proof.system === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
        typeof n.proof.statement === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
        n.proof.system.trim().length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
        n.proof.statement.trim().length &gt; 0
    ),
    &quot;FORM_EXTERNAL_PROOF_INCOMPLETE&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Structural graph is analyzable&quot;,
    structural.nodes.length === 0 || structural.components &gt;= 1,
    &quot;FORM_GRAPH_NOT_ANALYZABLE&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Roots are identifiable&quot;,
    Array.isArray(structural.roots),
    &quot;FORM_NO_ROOTS&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Leaves are identifiable&quot;,
    Array.isArray(structural.leaves),
    &quot;FORM_NO_LEAVES&quot;
  );

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * v0.8.4 — PROVENANCE/SCOPE EXTENSION, GRUPPO 7 + GRUPPO 4
   * (vedi design doc condiviso: &quot;Estensione ADEC — Provenienza,
   * Trasparenza e Scope delle Affermazioni&quot;)
   *
   * Entrambi i check sotto sono deliberatamente SCORATI (non
   * relegati a un layer informativo) perché, per costruzione,
   * scattano solo su un&#39;incoerenza/omissione dichiarata
   * esplicitamente nel JSON fornito — mai per la semplice
   * assenza di un campo facoltativo su una teoria che non lo usa
   * affatto. Zero falsi positivi retroattivi su teorie pre-v0.8.4.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const byId = new Map(nodes.map(n =&gt; [n.id, n]));

  /*
   * GRUPPO 7 — measurement_relationship
   * ------------------------------------------------------------
   * Campo facoltativo per nodo: additive | substitutive |
   * overlapping | unclear. Un nodo che aggrega più di una
   * dipendenza (tipicamente più meccanismi/componenti) dovrebbe
   * dichiarare come queste si combinano tra loro — altrimenti il
   * lettore deve indovinarlo dalla prosa.
   *
   * Trigger: nodo di tipo theorem/proposition/corollary con 2+
   * dipendenze e nessun measurement_relationship dichiarato (o
   * valore fuori vocabolario). Non scatta su nodi con 0-1 dep,
   * quindi non penalizza teorie preesistenti che non aggregano
   * nulla.
   */
  const MEASUREMENT_RELATIONSHIPS = new Set([
    &quot;additive&quot;,
    &quot;substitutive&quot;,
    &quot;overlapping&quot;,
    &quot;unclear&quot;
  ]);

  const multiDepClaimNodes = nodes.filter(n =&gt;
    [&quot;theorem&quot;, &quot;proposition&quot;, &quot;corollary&quot;].includes(n.kind) &amp;&amp;
    Array.isArray(n.deps) &amp;&amp;
    n.deps.length &gt;= 2
  );

  check(
    &quot;Multi-dependency claims declare measurement_relationship&quot;,
    multiDepClaimNodes.every(n =&gt;
      MEASUREMENT_RELATIONSHIPS.has(n.measurement_relationship)
    ),
    &quot;G_MULTI_MECHANISM_RELATIONSHIP_UNSPECIFIED&quot;
  );

  /*
   * GRUPPO 4 — claim_scope (versione debole, cross-node)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------
   * Campo facoltativo per nodo: existence | magnitude |
   * prediction. Segnala solo l&#39;ESCALATION DICHIARATA: un nodo
   * downstream con claim_scope magnitude/prediction i cui UNICI
   * dep con claim_scope dichiarato sono tutti existence. Non
   * analizza la prosa (description) — solo i campi strutturati,
   * come da decisione di design &quot;il motore non finge di capire
   * il linguaggio naturale&quot;. Non scatta se il downstream non
   * dichiara claim_scope, se nessun dep dichiara claim_scope, o
   * se almeno un dep ha claim_scope magnitude/prediction (in tal
   * caso l&#39;escalation potrebbe essere legittimamente supportata
   * da quell&#39;altra dipendenza).
   */
  const CLAIM_SCOPES = new Set([&quot;existence&quot;, &quot;magnitude&quot;, &quot;prediction&quot;]);

  const scopeEscalationNodes = nodes.filter(n =&gt; {
    if (![&quot;magnitude&quot;, &quot;prediction&quot;].includes(n.claim_scope)) return false;
    if (!Array.isArray(n.deps) || n.deps.length === 0) return false;

    const scopedDeps = n.deps
      .map(depId =&gt; byId.get(depId))
      .filter(dep =&gt; dep &amp;&amp; CLAIM_SCOPES.has(dep.claim_scope));

    if (scopedDeps.length === 0) return false;

    return scopedDeps.every(dep =&gt; dep.claim_scope === &quot;existence&quot;);
  });

  check(
    &quot;No declared claim_scope escalation from existence-only dependencies&quot;,
    scopeEscalationNodes.length === 0,
    &quot;G_SCOPE_ESCALATION&quot;
  );

  /*
   * GRUPPO 6 — competitive_hypotheses (design doc, Gruppo 6)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------
   * Campo facoltativo per nodo: array di
   * { hypothesis: string, distinguishing_test: string }.
   *
   * IMPORTANTE (limite dichiarato, non un bug): questo check
   * verifica SOLO la coerenza interna — se dichiari un&#39;ipotesi
   * competitiva, hai anche il test che la distingue dalla tua?
   * NON può verificare &quot;hai dimenticato un&#39;ipotesi alternativa
   * nota in letteratura&quot;: richiederebbe conoscenza di dominio
   * esterna che il motore non ha e non deve fingere di avere.
   * Coerente con &quot;il motore verifica solo quello che può vedere
   * con certezza&quot;, già seguito per gli altri check di questo
   * file.
   *
   * Trigger: solo su nodi che GIÀ dichiarano competitive_hypotheses
   * con almeno una entry con hypothesis valorizzata — zero impatto
   * su teorie che non usano affatto il campo.
   */
  const hypothesisIncompleteNodeIds = [];

  nodes.forEach(n =&gt; {
    if (!Array.isArray(n.competitive_hypotheses)) return;
    const hasIncomplete = n.competitive_hypotheses.some(h =&gt;
      h &amp;&amp; typeof h === &quot;object&quot; &amp;&amp;
      typeof h.hypothesis === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; h.hypothesis.trim().length &gt; 0 &amp;&amp;
      (typeof h.distinguishing_test !== &quot;string&quot; || h.distinguishing_test.trim().length === 0)
    );
    if (hasIncomplete) hypothesisIncompleteNodeIds.push(n.id);
  });

  check(
    &quot;Competitive hypotheses declare a distinguishing test&quot;,
    hypothesisIncompleteNodeIds.length === 0,
    &quot;G_HYPOTHESIS_WITHOUT_TEST&quot;
  );

  const total = checks.length;
  const score = total === 0 ? 100 : Math.round((passed / total) * 100);

  return {
    checks,
    passed,
    total,
    score,
    grade: gradeFor(score),
    proofTheorems: proofBearingNodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;theorem&quot; &amp;&amp; (hasValidationEvidence(n) || hasExternalFormalProof(n))),
    theoremNodes,
    proofBearingNodes,
    proofEvidenceNodes,
    externalProofNodes,
    proofCoverage: {
      total: proofBearingNodes.length,
      withEvidence: proofEvidenceNodes.length,
      withExternalFormalProof: externalProofNodes.length,
      withoutProof: proofBearingNodes.filter(n =&gt; !n.proof).map(n =&gt; n.id),
      theoremTotal: theoremNodes.length,
      theoremWithProof: theoremNodes.filter(n =&gt; hasValidationEvidence(n) || hasExternalFormalProof(n)).length,
      propositionTotal: proofBearingNodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;proposition&quot;).length,
      propositionWithProof: proofBearingNodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;proposition&quot; &amp;&amp; (hasValidationEvidence(n) || hasExternalFormalProof(n))).length
    },
    measurementRelationshipMissing: multiDepClaimNodes
      .filter(n =&gt; !MEASUREMENT_RELATIONSHIPS.has(n.measurement_relationship))
      .map(n =&gt; n.id),
    scopeEscalationNodes: scopeEscalationNodes.map(n =&gt; n.id),
    hypothesisIncompleteNodeIds
  };
}


/*
 * ============================================================
 * v0.8.5 — PROVENANCE EXTENSION, GRUPPO 1 del design doc
   * &quot;Estensione ADEC — Provenienza, Trasparenza e Scope delle
   * Affermazioni&quot; (finanziamenti / conflitti di interesse).
   *
   * A differenza di validateFormal(), questo layer è
   * DELIBERATAMENTE ESCLUSO da frameworkScore, validationScore
   * ed engineIntegrityScore — vedi validateTheory(). Un
   * finanziamento non dichiarato o un COI incongruente sono
   * problemi di TRASPARENZA PROCEDURALE, non prova che
   * un&#39;affermazione sia falsa: penalizzarli nel punteggio
   * complessivo tratterebbe allo stesso modo una teoria
   * falsificata e uno studio onesto ma poco meticoloso nella
   * burocrazia. Il punteggio qui calcolato è solo diagnostico,
   * mostrato separatamente, mai sommato altrove.
   *
   * Schema dati atteso (tutti campi facoltativi):
   *   - data.authors: [{ name, affiliation, conflict_of_interest }]
   *   - node.peer_reviewed: boolean
   *   - node.funding: string | string[]
   *   - node.funder_relationship_to_claim: &quot;independent&quot; |
   *       &quot;funder_manufactures_tested_product&quot; |
   *       &quot;funder_has_financial_stake&quot; | &quot;unclear&quot;
   *
   * NOTA IMPORTANTE (autocertificazione): peer_reviewed e
   * funder_relationship_to_claim sono dichiarati dall&#39;autore
   * della teoria — il motore, essendo client-side e offline,
   * non li verifica contro alcuna fonte esterna. I messaggi
   * dei check lo ricordano esplicitamente.
   * ============================================================
   */
  function validateProvenance(data, structural) {

    const nodes = structural.nodes;
    const authors = Array.isArray(data.authors) ? data.authors : [];

    const checks = [];
    let passed = 0;

    function check(label, condition, code = null) {
      const ok = !!condition;
      checks.push({ label, passed: ok, code });
      if (ok) passed++;
      return ok;
    }

    function hasFunding(n) {
      if (typeof n.funding === &quot;string&quot;) return n.funding.trim().length &gt; 0;
      if (Array.isArray(n.funding)) return n.funding.some(f =&gt; typeof f === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; f.trim().length &gt; 0);
      return false;
    }

    function fundingText(n) {
      if (typeof n.funding === &quot;string&quot;) return n.funding;
      if (Array.isArray(n.funding)) return n.funding.filter(f =&gt; typeof f === &quot;string&quot;).join(&quot; ; &quot;);
      return &quot;&quot;;
    }

    function normalize(s) {
      return String(s || &quot;&quot;).toLowerCase().trim();
    }

    const FUNDER_RELATIONSHIPS = new Set([
      &quot;independent&quot;,
      &quot;funder_manufactures_tested_product&quot;,
      &quot;funder_has_financial_stake&quot;,
      &quot;unclear&quot;
    ]);

    /*
     * CHECK 1 — PROV_PEER_REVIEWED_FUNDING_UNDISCLOSED
     * peer_reviewed: true + funding assente/vuoto.
     * Autocertificazione: peer_reviewed non è verificato dal motore.
     */
    const peerReviewedUndisclosed = nodes.filter(n =&gt;
      n.peer_reviewed === true &amp;&amp; !hasFunding(n)
    );

    check(
      &quot;Peer-reviewed nodes disclose a funding source&quot;,
      peerReviewedUndisclosed.length === 0,
      &quot;PROV_PEER_REVIEWED_FUNDING_UNDISCLOSED&quot;
    );

    /*
     * CHECK 2 — G_INDUSTRY_FUNDED_FIT_UNDISCLOSED_RISK
     * Nodo experiment con funder_has_financial_stake e nessuna
     * nota esplicita del rischio nella description. Puramente
     * informativo: uno studio finanziato dall&#39;industria può
     * comunque essere onesto, ma il lettore deve poterlo sapere.
     */
    const industryRiskPattern = /finanz|conflitto|interesse|funding|conflict of interest|financial stake/i;

    const industryFundedRisk = nodes.filter(n =&gt;
      n.kind === &quot;experiment&quot; &amp;&amp;
      n.funder_relationship_to_claim === &quot;funder_has_financial_stake&quot; &amp;&amp;
      !industryRiskPattern.test(String(n.description || &quot;&quot;))
    );

    check(
      &quot;Industry-funded experiments with a financial stake flag the risk in their description&quot;,
      industryFundedRisk.length === 0,
      &quot;G_INDUSTRY_FUNDED_FIT_UNDISCLOSED_RISK&quot;
    );

    /*
     * CHECK 3 — G_VAGUE_FUNDING_DISCLOSURE
     * &quot;supportato da sovvenzioni&quot; / &quot;partner industriali&quot; senza
     * un nome proprio riconoscibile — distinto dall&#39;assenza
     * totale di dichiarazione (che è il check 1).
     */
    const vaguePattern = /\b(sovvenzion\w*|grant\w*|partner\s+industrial\w*|industry\s+partner\w*|supportat[oa]\s+da|sponsor\w*)\b/i;

    function isVagueFunding(text) {
      const s = String(text || &quot;&quot;);
      if (!s.trim() || !vaguePattern.test(s)) return false;
      // Se compare un token con iniziale maiuscola (candidato nome
      // proprio: azienda, ente, sigla) non è considerato vago.
      const words = s.trim().split(/\s+/);
      const hasNamedToken = words.some((w, i) =&gt;
        i &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; /^[A-ZÀ-Ý][a-zà-ÿ]{2,}$/.test(w.replace(/[.,;:]$/, &quot;&quot;))
      );
      return !hasNamedToken;
    }

    const vagueFundingNodes = nodes.filter(n =&gt; hasFunding(n) &amp;&amp; isVagueFunding(fundingText(n)));

    check(
      &quot;Declared funding sources are specific, not vague&quot;,
      vagueFundingNodes.length === 0,
      &quot;G_VAGUE_FUNDING_DISCLOSURE&quot;
    );

    /*
     * CHECK 4 — PROV_COI_INCONGRUENT
     * Autore con conflict_of_interest &quot;nessuno&quot;/assente la cui
     * affiliazione compare (case-insensitive, substring match —
     * niente fuzzy matching, per restare deterministico) nel
     * testo di funding di un nodo. Match semplice: pochi colpi
     * sicuri preferibili a un match troppo permissivo che genera
     * falsi positivi.
     */
    function declaresNoCoi(text) {
      const n = normalize(text);
      return n === &quot;&quot; || [&quot;none&quot;, &quot;nessuno&quot;, &quot;nessun conflitto&quot;, &quot;n/a&quot;, &quot;no&quot;].includes(n);
    }

    const incongruentAuthors = authors.filter(author =&gt; {
      if (!declaresNoCoi(author.conflict_of_interest)) return false;
      const affiliation = normalize(author.affiliation);
      if (affiliation.length &lt; 3) return false;
      return nodes.some(n =&gt; normalize(fundingText(n)).includes(affiliation));
    });

    check(
      &quot;No author with an undeclared COI matching a funding source affiliation&quot;,
      incongruentAuthors.length === 0,
      &quot;PROV_COI_INCONGRUENT&quot;
    );

    const total = checks.length;
    const score = total === 0 ? 100 : Math.round((passed / total) * 100);

    const messages = [];

    if (peerReviewedUndisclosed.length &gt; 0) {
      messages.push(
        `Provenance (non scorato — autocertificazione, non verificata dal motore): nodo/i peer-reviewed senza finanziamento dichiarato: ${peerReviewedUndisclosed.map(n =&gt; `&quot;${n.id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`
      );
    }
    if (industryFundedRisk.length &gt; 0) {
      messages.push(
        `Provenance (non scorato): esperimento/i con finanziatore a interesse diretto, rischio non segnalato in description: ${industryFundedRisk.map(n =&gt; `&quot;${n.id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`
      );
    }
    if (vagueFundingNodes.length &gt; 0) {
      messages.push(
        `Provenance (non scorato): finanziamento dichiarato in modo vago (nessun nome riconoscibile) su ${vagueFundingNodes.map(n =&gt; `&quot;${n.id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`
      );
    }
    if (incongruentAuthors.length &gt; 0) {
      messages.push(
        `Provenance (non scorato): COI dichiarato come assente ma affiliazione coincidente con un finanziatore per: ${incongruentAuthors.map(a =&gt; `&quot;${a.name || &quot;(nome non specificato)&quot;}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`
      );
    }

    return {
      checks,
      passed,
      total,
      score,
      grade: gradeFor(score),
      messages,
      peerReviewedUndisclosed: peerReviewedUndisclosed.map(n =&gt; n.id),
      industryFundedRisk: industryFundedRisk.map(n =&gt; n.id),
      vagueFundingNodes: vagueFundingNodes.map(n =&gt; n.id),
      incongruentAuthors: incongruentAuthors.map(a =&gt; a.name || &quot;(nome non specificato)&quot;)
    };
  }


function validateSelf() {

  const checks = [];
  let passed = 0;
  const diagnostics = [];

  function check(label, condition, detail = &quot;&quot;) {
    const ok = !!condition;

    checks.push({
      label,
      passed: ok,
      detail
    });

    if (ok) {
      passed++;
    } else {
      diagnostics.push({
        label,
        detail: detail || &quot;Nessun dettaglio diagnostico disponibile.&quot;
      });
    }
  }

  /*
   * Self-validation is validation of the DECLARED self-model.
   * It is not a proof of the JavaScript implementation and it is
   * not a metatheoretic proof of ADEC consistency.
   */
  const selfModel =
    PRESETS[&quot;ADEC Self-Model&quot;] ||
    PRESETS[&quot;ADEC Self-Meta Validation Model&quot;];

  check(
    &quot;ADEC self-model available&quot;,
    !!selfModel &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(selfModel.nodes),
    &quot;Il preset interno deve contenere un array nodes valido.&quot;
  );

  let structural = null;
  let semantic = null;
  let formal = null;

  if (selfModel) {
    structural = validateStructural(selfModel);
    semantic = validateSemantic(selfModel, structural);
    formal = validateFormal(selfModel, structural);
  }

  check(
    &quot;Self structural layer available&quot;,
    !!structural,
    &quot;Il livello Structural deve poter essere eseguito sul self-model.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self semantic layer available&quot;,
    !!semantic,
    &quot;Il livello Semantic deve poter essere eseguito sul self-model.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self formal layer available&quot;,
    !!formal,
    &quot;Il livello Formal deve poter essere eseguito sul self-model.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self structural score bounded&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(structural.score) &amp;&amp;
      structural.score &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; structural.score &lt;= 100,
    structural
      ? `Structural score=${structural.score}; intervallo ammesso 0–100.`
      : &quot;Structural result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self semantic score bounded&quot;,
    !!semantic &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(semantic.score) &amp;&amp;
      semantic.score &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; semantic.score &lt;= 100,
    semantic
      ? `Semantic score=${semantic.score}; intervallo ammesso 0–100.`
      : &quot;Semantic result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self formal score bounded&quot;,
    !!formal &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(formal.score) &amp;&amp;
      formal.score &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; formal.score &lt;= 100,
    formal
      ? `Formal score=${formal.score}; intervallo ammesso 0–100.`
      : &quot;Formal result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self structural grade coherent&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; structural.grade === gradeFor(structural.score),
    structural
      ? `Grade dichiarato=${structural.grade}; grade calcolato=${gradeFor(structural.score)}.`
      : &quot;Structural result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self semantic grade coherent&quot;,
    !!semantic &amp;&amp; semantic.grade === gradeFor(semantic.score),
    semantic
      ? `Grade dichiarato=${semantic.grade}; grade calcolato=${gradeFor(semantic.score)}.`
      : &quot;Semantic result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self formal grade coherent&quot;,
    !!formal &amp;&amp; formal.grade === gradeFor(formal.score),
    formal
      ? `Grade dichiarato=${formal.grade}; grade calcolato=${gradeFor(formal.score)}.`
      : &quot;Formal result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self-model has no structural errors&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; structural.errors.length === 0,
    structural
      ? structural.errors.length === 0
        ? &quot;Nessun errore strutturale nel self-model.&quot;
        : `${structural.errors.length} errore/i strutturale/i: ${structural.errors.join(&quot; | &quot;)}`
      : &quot;Structural result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  /*
   * IMPORTANT:
   * These two checks are deliberately diagnostic rather than opaque.
   * A failure means that the declared self-model has not itself passed
   * every semantic/formal rule. It does NOT mean that ADEC is inconsistent.
   */
  const semanticFailures = semantic
    ? semantic.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed)
    : [];

  const formalFailures = formal
    ? formal.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed)
    : [];

  const semanticDetail = semanticFailures.length
    ? semanticFailures
        .map(c =&gt; `${c.label}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : &quot;&quot;}`)
        .join(&quot; | &quot;)
    : semantic
      ? `Tutti i ${semantic.total} controlli semantici del self-model sono superati.`
      : &quot;Semantic result non disponibile.&quot;;

  const formalDetail = formalFailures.length
    ? formalFailures
        .map(c =&gt; `${c.label}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : &quot;&quot;}`)
        .join(&quot; | &quot;)
    : formal
      ? `Tutti i ${formal.total} controlli formali del self-model sono superati.`
      : &quot;Formal result non disponibile.&quot;;

  check(
    &quot;Self-model semantic checks pass&quot;,
    !!semantic &amp;&amp; semanticFailures.length === 0,
    semanticDetail
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self-model formal representation checks pass&quot;,
    !!formal &amp;&amp; formalFailures.length === 0,
    formalDetail
  );

  const theoremNodes = formal?.theoremNodes || [];
  const proofTheorems = formal?.proofTheorems || [];

  check(
    &quot;Self-model theorem proof/evidence records present&quot;,
    !!formal &amp;&amp; theoremNodes.every(
      n =&gt; proofTheorems.some(p =&gt; p.id === n.id)
    ),
    theoremNodes.length
      ? `Teoremi=${theoremNodes.map(n =&gt; n.id).join(&quot;, &quot;)}; record trovati=${proofTheorems.map(n =&gt; n.id).join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}.`
      : &quot;Nessun theorem nel self-model.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self-model graph is acyclic&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; structural.cycles.length === 0,
    structural
      ? structural.cycles.length === 0
        ? &quot;Nessun ciclo di dipendenza nel self-model.&quot;
        : `${structural.cycles.length} ciclo/i rilevato/i.`
      : &quot;Structural result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self-model foundation coverage bounded&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; structural.foundationCoverage &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp;
      structural.foundationCoverage &lt;= 100,
    structural
      ? `Foundation coverage=${structural.foundationCoverage}%; intervallo ammesso 0–100%.`
      : &quot;Structural result non disponibile.&quot;
  );

  const selfNodes = selfModel?.nodes || [];

  check(
    &quot;Self-model recognizes self-validation nodes&quot;,
    selfNodes.some(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;self_validation&quot;),
    `Nodi self_validation trovati: ${selfNodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;self_validation&quot;).map(n =&gt; n.id).join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}.`
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self-model recognizes meta-validation nodes&quot;,
    selfNodes.some(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;meta_validation&quot;),
    `Nodi meta_validation trovati: ${selfNodes.filter(n =&gt; n.kind === &quot;meta_validation&quot;).map(n =&gt; n.id).join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}.`
  );

  /* Additional diagnostics, not counted as pass/fail controls. */
  const diagnosticSummary = {
    semanticFailures,
    formalFailures,
    semanticPassed: semantic?.passed ?? 0,
    semanticTotal: semantic?.total ?? 0,
    formalPassed: formal?.passed ?? 0,
    formalTotal: formal?.total ?? 0,
    theoremNodes: theoremNodes.map(n =&gt; n.id),
    proofEvidenceNodes: formal?.proofEvidenceNodes?.map(n =&gt; n.id) || [],
    externalProofNodes: formal?.externalProofNodes?.map(n =&gt; n.id) || []
  };

  const total = checks.length;
  const score = total === 0 ? 100 : Math.round((passed / total) * 100);

  return {
    checks,
    passed,
    total,
    score,
    grade: gradeFor(score),
    modelTitle: selfModel?.title || &quot;ADEC Self-Model&quot;,
    diagnostics: diagnosticSummary
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   META VALIDATION
   ========================================================= */

function validateMeta(
  structural,
  semantic,
  formal,
  self,
  mathematical
) {

  const checks = [];
  let passed = 0;

  function check(label, condition) {
    const ok = !!condition;
    checks.push({ label, passed: ok });
    if (ok) passed++;
  }

  check(
    &quot;Core validation layers available&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; !!semantic &amp;&amp; !!formal &amp;&amp; !!self
  );

  check(
    &quot;Structural Validation executed&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(structural.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Semantic Validation executed&quot;,
    !!semantic &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(semantic.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Formal Validation executed&quot;,
    !!formal &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(formal.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Self layer executed&quot;,
    !!self &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(self.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Layer grades coherent&quot;,
    [structural, semantic, formal, self].every(
      r =&gt; !!r &amp;&amp; r.grade === gradeFor(r.score)
    )
  );

  check(
    &quot;Structural graph finite&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(structural.nodes.length) &amp;&amp;
    Number.isFinite(structural.edges.length)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Layer scores finite&quot;,
    [structural, semantic, formal, self].every(
      r =&gt; !!r &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(r.score)
    )
  );

  check(
    &quot;Layer scores bounded&quot;,
    [structural, semantic, formal, self].every(
      r =&gt; !!r &amp;&amp; r.score &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; r.score &lt;= 100
    )
  );

  check(
    &quot;Structural foundation coverage bounded&quot;,
    !!structural &amp;&amp; structural.foundationCoverage &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp;
    structural.foundationCoverage &lt;= 100
  );

  check(
    &quot;Mathematical analysis layer available&quot;,
    !!mathematical &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(mathematical.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Mathematical score bounded&quot;,
    !!mathematical &amp;&amp; mathematical.score &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; mathematical.score &lt;= 100
  );

  check(
    &quot;Algebraic analysis layer available&quot;,
    !!mathematical?.algebraic &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(mathematical.algebraic.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Logical analysis layer available&quot;,
    !!mathematical?.logical &amp;&amp; Number.isFinite(mathematical.logical.score)
  );

  check(
    &quot;Analysis layer grades coherent&quot;,
    !!mathematical &amp;&amp;
      !!mathematical.algebraic &amp;&amp;
      !!mathematical.logical &amp;&amp;
      mathematical.grade === gradeFor(mathematical.score) &amp;&amp;
      mathematical.algebraic.grade === gradeFor(mathematical.algebraic.score) &amp;&amp;
      mathematical.logical.grade === gradeFor(mathematical.logical.score)
  );

  const total = checks.length;
  const score = total === 0 ? 100 : Math.round((passed / total) * 100);

  return {
    checks,
    passed,
    total,
    score,
    grade: gradeFor(score)
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   GLOBAL VALIDATION
   ========================================================= */


// ==================== STRESS ORACLE v0.8.2 ====================
/*
 * Confronta i codici di finding DICHIARATI come attesi in
 * data.stress_test.expected con quelli EFFETTIVAMENTE rilevati dal
 * motore, categoria per categoria. Sostituisce il precedente
 * &quot;layer coverage&quot; (che misurava solo se una layer si era attivata,
 * non SE avesse trovato esattamente ciò che il fixture si aspettava).
 *
 * Formato atteso per ciascuna categoria: un ARRAY di codici stringa,
 * es. expected.semantic_failures = [&quot;SEM_BAD_KIND&quot;,&quot;SEM_EMPTY_NAME&quot;,...].
 * Se una categoria non è un array (assente, o booleano legacy), viene
 * saltata nel confronto puntuale: il &quot;layer coverage&quot; resta comunque
 * disponibile come fallback per compatibilità con fixture più vecchi
 * che non dichiarano codici espliciti.
 *
 * self_meta resta intenzionalmente FUORI dal confronto per codici:
 * self/meta validano la coerenza interna del MOTORE stesso, non il
 * contenuto della teoria, quindi non producono &quot;finding&quot; per-item da
 * confrontare con un elenco atteso — solo un punteggio pass/fail.
 */
function evaluateStressOracle(data, result) {

  if (!data || !data.stress_test || !data.stress_test.expected) return null;

  const expected = data.stress_test.expected;

  const detectedCodes = {
    structural: Array.from(new Set(result?.structural?.errorCodes || [])),
    semantic: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.semantic?.checks || [])
        .filter(c =&gt; c &amp;&amp; c.passed === false &amp;&amp; c.code)
        .map(c =&gt; c.code)
    )),
    formal: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.formal?.checks || [])
        .filter(c =&gt; c &amp;&amp; c.passed === false &amp;&amp; c.code)
        .map(c =&gt; c.code)
    )),
    mathematical: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.mathematical?.results || [])
        .map(r =&gt; r &amp;&amp; r.status)
        .filter(Boolean)
    )),
    algebraic: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.mathematical?.algebraic?.results || [])
        .map(r =&gt; r &amp;&amp; r.status)
        .filter(Boolean)
    )),
    logical: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.mathematical?.logical?.findings || [])
        .map(f =&gt; f &amp;&amp; f.status)
        .filter(Boolean)
    )),
    provenance: Array.from(new Set(
      (result?.provenance?.checks || [])
        .filter(c =&gt; c &amp;&amp; c.passed === false &amp;&amp; c.code)
        .map(c =&gt; c.code)
    ))
  };

  const categoryKeyMap = {
    structural_errors: &quot;structural&quot;,
    semantic_failures: &quot;semantic&quot;,
    formal_failures: &quot;formal&quot;,
    mathematical: &quot;mathematical&quot;,
    algebraic: &quot;algebraic&quot;,
    logical: &quot;logical&quot;,
    provenance_flags: &quot;provenance&quot;
  };

  const categories = {};
  let totalExpected = 0;
  let totalMissing = 0;
  let hasCodeBasedExpectations = false;

  for (const [expectedKey, resultKey] of Object.entries(categoryKeyMap)) {

    const expectedValue = expected[expectedKey];
    if (!Array.isArray(expectedValue)) continue;

    hasCodeBasedExpectations = true;

    const expectedSet = Array.from(new Set(expectedValue));
    const detectedSet = detectedCodes[resultKey] || [];

    const detected = expectedSet.filter(code =&gt; detectedSet.includes(code));
    const missing = expectedSet.filter(code =&gt; !detectedSet.includes(code));
    const unexpected = detectedSet.filter(code =&gt; !expectedSet.includes(code));

    totalExpected += expectedSet.length;
    totalMissing += missing.length;

    categories[resultKey] = { expected: expectedSet, detected, missing, unexpected };

  }

  const selfMetaExpected = !!expected.self_meta;
  const selfMetaOk = Number(result?.self?.score) === 100 &amp;&amp; Number(result?.meta?.score) === 100;

  /* Layer coverage legacy: resta disponibile come informazione
   * complementare (utile anche in modalità oracolo, per capire se una
   * layer non si è mossa affatto piuttosto che aver mancato un target
   * specifico), ma non decide più da sola il verdetto quando l&#39;oracolo
   * per codici è disponibile. */
  const legacyChecks = [
    [&quot;structural&quot;, (result?.structural?.errors || []).length &gt; 0],
    [&quot;semantic&quot;, (result?.semantic?.checks || []).some(c =&gt; c &amp;&amp; c.passed === false)],
    [&quot;formal&quot;, (result?.formal?.checks || []).some(c =&gt; c &amp;&amp; c.passed === false)],
    [&quot;mathematical&quot;, Number(result?.mathematical?.falseNumeric || 0) &gt; 0 || Number(result?.mathematical?.falseComplex || 0) &gt; 0 || Number(result?.mathematical?.review || 0) &gt; 0],
    [&quot;algebraic&quot;, Number(result?.mathematical?.algebraic?.failures || 0) &gt; 0 || Number(result?.mathematical?.algebraic?.review || 0) &gt; 0],
    [&quot;logical&quot;, Number(result?.mathematical?.logical?.failures || 0) &gt; 0 || Number(result?.mathematical?.logical?.warnings || 0) &gt; 0],
    [&quot;self_meta&quot;, selfMetaOk]
  ];
  const layersPassed = legacyChecks.filter(x =&gt; x[1]).length;
  const layersTotal = legacyChecks.length;

  const selfMetaMissing = selfMetaExpected &amp;&amp; !selfMetaOk;

  return {
    hasCodeBasedExpectations,
    categories,
    totalExpected,
    totalMissing,
    selfMetaExpected,
    selfMetaOk,
    selfMetaMissing,
    oracleMissingTotal: totalMissing + (selfMetaMissing ? 1 : 0),
    layerCoverage: layersTotal ? Math.round(100 * layersPassed / layersTotal) : 0,
    layersPassed,
    layersTotal
  };

}

function validateTheory(data) {
  const structural = validateStructural(data);
  const semantic = validateSemantic(data, structural);
  const formal = validateFormal(data, structural);
  const provenance = validateProvenance(data, structural);
  const mathematical = analyzeMathematicalProofs(data);
  const self = validateSelf();
  const meta = validateMeta(structural, semantic, formal, self, mathematical);

  /*
   * SCORING MODEL — v0.8.1 CORRECTED
   *
   * Three scores are deliberately separated:
   *
   * 1) frameworkScore:
   *    quality of the ADEC representation itself
   *    (Structural + Semantic + Formal + Self + Meta).
   *
   * 2) validationScore:
   *    consistency of the CONTENT actually analysed
   *    (Structural + Semantic + Formal + Mathematical +
   *     Algebraic + Logical).
   *
   * 3) engineIntegrityScore:
   *    internal self/meta validation of the validator.
   *
   * This prevents a mathematical stress test containing false
   * equations from being reported simply as &quot;100 A+&quot; while
   * simultaneously preserving the meaning of a perfect
   * framework/self-validation score.
   */
  const frameworkScore = Math.round(
    structural.score * 0.30 +
    semantic.score * 0.25 +
    formal.score * 0.25 +
    self.score * 0.10 +
    meta.score * 0.10
  );

  const validationScore = Math.round(
    structural.score * 0.10 +
    semantic.score * 0.10 +
    formal.score * 0.10 +
    mathematical.score * 0.25 +
    mathematical.algebraic.score * 0.25 +
    mathematical.logical.score * 0.20
  );

  const engineIntegrityScore = Math.round(
    self.score * 0.50 +
    meta.score * 0.50
  );

  const score = frameworkScore;
  const grade = gradeFor(score);
  const validationGrade = gradeFor(validationScore);
  const engineIntegrityGrade = gradeFor(engineIntegrityScore);

  /*
   * STRESS-TEST SEMANTICS
   *
   * A stress test deliberately contains invalid structures, semantic
   * defects, formal defects and mathematical/logical counterexamples.
   * Those findings are expected test stimuli; they must not be confused
   * with a failure of the validator itself.
   *
   * The explicit JSON field `validation_mode: &quot;stress_test&quot;` is preferred.
   * For backward compatibility with the supplied v0.7.9 stress fixture,
   * the title is accepted only as a legacy fallback.
   */
  const isStressTest =
    data.validation_mode === &quot;stress_test&quot; ||
    /COMPLETE ALL-LAYERS STRESS TEST/i.test(String(data.title || &quot;&quot;));

  const stressCoverage = {
    structural: structural.errors.length &gt; 0,
    semantic: semantic.checks.some(c =&gt; !c.passed),
    formal: formal.checks.some(c =&gt; !c.passed),
    mathematical: mathematical.falseNumeric &gt; 0 || mathematical.falseComplex &gt; 0 || mathematical.review &gt; 0,
    algebraic: mathematical.algebraic.failures &gt; 0 || mathematical.algebraic.review &gt; 0,
    logical: mathematical.logical.failures &gt; 0 || mathematical.logical.warnings &gt; 0,
    selfMeta: self.score === 100 &amp;&amp; meta.score === 100
  };

  const stressChecks = Object.values(stressCoverage);
  const stressPassed = stressChecks.filter(Boolean).length;
  const stressTotal = stressChecks.length;
  const stressTestCoverage = Math.round((stressPassed / stressTotal) * 100);

  /*
   * v0.8.2 — l&#39;oracolo per codici (quando il fixture dichiara
   * data.stress_test.expected con array di codici) decide il verdetto
   * al posto del solo &quot;layer coverage&quot;: una layer esercitata non basta,
   * deve aver rilevato ESATTAMENTE i finding attesi. Se il fixture non
   * dichiara codici (o non è affatto uno stress test), il comportamento
   * legacy basato sul layer coverage resta invariato.
   */
  const stressOracle = isStressTest ? evaluateStressOracle(data, { structural, semantic, formal, provenance, mathematical, self, meta }) : null;

  const stressOutcome = !isStressTest
    ? null
    : (stressOracle &amp;&amp; stressOracle.hasCodeBasedExpectations)
      ? (stressOracle.oracleMissingTotal === 0 ? &quot;PASS&quot; : &quot;PARTIAL&quot;)
      : (stressTestCoverage === 100 ? &quot;PASS&quot; : &quot;INCOMPLETE&quot;);

  const stressTestVerdict = !isStressTest
    ? null
    : stressOutcome === &quot;PASS&quot;
      ? (stressOracle &amp;&amp; stressOracle.hasCodeBasedExpectations
          ? &quot;PASS — ALL EXPECTED FINDINGS DETECTED&quot;
          : &quot;PASS — ALL TARGETED LAYERS EXERCISED&quot;)
      : stressOutcome === &quot;PARTIAL&quot;
        ? `PARTIAL — ${stressOracle.oracleMissingTotal} EXPECTED FINDING(S) NOT DETECTED (layer coverage ${stressTestCoverage}%)`
        : &quot;INCOMPLETE — SOME TARGETED LAYERS NOT EXERCISED&quot;;

  const warnings = [...structural.warnings];
  const infos = [];

  for (const check of semantic.checks) if (!check.passed) warnings.push(`Semantic check fallito: ${check.label}.`);

  for (const check of formal.checks) {
    if (check.passed) continue;
    if (check.code === &quot;FORM_INVALID_EDGE&quot;) {
      const invalidEdges = structural.edges.filter(e =&gt; e.invalid).map(e =&gt; `&quot;${e.to}&quot; → &quot;${e.from}&quot;`);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): dipendenza inesistente su ${invalidEdges.join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`);
    } else if (check.code === &quot;FORM_CIRCULAR_DEP&quot;) {
      const cycleList = structural.cycles.map(c =&gt; c.join(&quot; → &quot;)).join(&quot; | &quot;);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): ciclo/i rilevato/i: ${cycleList}.`);
    } else if (check.code === &quot;FORM_SELF_DEP&quot;) {
      const selfDepIds = structural.nodes.filter(n =&gt; Array.isArray(n.deps) &amp;&amp; n.deps.includes(n.id)).map(n =&gt; n.id);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): auto-dipendenza su ${selfDepIds.map(id =&gt; `&quot;${id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;)}.`);
    } else if (check.code === &quot;G_MULTI_MECHANISM_RELATIONSHIP_UNSPECIFIED&quot;) {
      const ids = formal.measurementRelationshipMissing.map(id =&gt; `&quot;${id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): nodo/i con 2+ dipendenze senza measurement_relationship dichiarato (additive/substitutive/overlapping/unclear): ${ids}.`);
    } else if (check.code === &quot;G_SCOPE_ESCALATION&quot;) {
      const ids = formal.scopeEscalationNodes.map(id =&gt; `&quot;${id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): claim_scope escalation dichiarata (dipendenza/e &quot;existence&quot; usata/e a supporto di un claim_scope magnitude/prediction) su ${ids}.`);
    } else if (check.code === &quot;G_HYPOTHESIS_WITHOUT_TEST&quot;) {
      const ids = formal.hypothesisIncompleteNodeIds.map(id =&gt; `&quot;${id}&quot;`).join(&quot;, &quot;);
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito (${check.label}): ipotesi competitiva dichiarata senza distinguishing_test su ${ids}.`);
    } else {
      warnings.push(`Formal representation check fallito: ${check.label}.`);
    }
  }

  if (mathematical.falseNumeric &gt; 0) warnings.push(`Mathematical consistency: ${mathematical.falseNumeric} uguaglianza/equazione numerica falsa rilevata.`);
  if (mathematical.falseComplex &gt; 0) warnings.push(`Mathematical consistency: ${mathematical.falseComplex} uguaglianza numerica complessa falsa rilevata.`);
  if (mathematical.tautologies &gt; 0) warnings.push(`Mathematical consistency: ${mathematical.tautologies} passaggio/i tautologico/i o algebricamente equivalente/i rilevato/i.`);
  if (mathematical.review &gt; 0) infos.push(`Mathematical consistency: ${mathematical.review} passaggio/i richiedono revisione matematica ulteriore.`);
  if (mathematical.algebraic.failures &gt; 0) warnings.push(`Algebraic consistency: ${mathematical.algebraic.failures} fallimento/i algebrico/i dimostrabile/i rilevato/i.`);
  if (mathematical.algebraic.review &gt; 0) infos.push(`Algebraic consistency: ${mathematical.algebraic.review} passaggio/i richiedono verifica algebrica ulteriore.`);
  if (mathematical.logical.failures &gt; 0) warnings.push(`Logical consistency: ${mathematical.logical.failures} possibile/i ragionamento/i circolare/i o logicamente critico/i rilevato/i.`);
  if (mathematical.logical.warnings &gt; 0) infos.push(`Logical consistency: ${mathematical.logical.warnings} anomalia/e o passaggio/i da revisionare.`);

  if (formal.proofCoverage.withoutProof.length &gt; 0) {
    infos.push(`Proof coverage: ${formal.proofCoverage.withoutProof.length}/${formal.proofCoverage.total} nodi proof-bearing non hanno un record proof/evidence.`);
  }

  if (self.passed &lt; self.total) warnings.push(`Self-Validation incompleta: ${self.passed}/${self.total} controlli superati.`);
  if (meta.passed &lt; meta.total) warnings.push(`Meta-Validation incompleta: ${meta.passed}/${meta.total} controlli superati.`);

  const status = isStressTest
    ? (stressOutcome === &quot;PASS&quot; ? &quot;STRESS TEST PASS&quot; : stressOutcome === &quot;PARTIAL&quot; ? &quot;STRESS TEST PARTIAL&quot; : &quot;STRESS TEST INCOMPLETE&quot;)
    : (structural.errors.length &gt; 0 ? &quot;INVALID&quot; : warnings.length &gt; 0 ? &quot;VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; : &quot;VALID&quot;);

  const mathematicalReviewRequired = mathematical.falseNumeric &gt; 0 || mathematical.falseComplex &gt; 0 || mathematical.logical.failures &gt; 0 || mathematical.logical.warnings &gt; 0 || mathematical.algebraic.failures &gt; 0 || mathematical.review &gt; 0 || mathematical.algebraic.review &gt; 0;

  const assessment = isStressTest
    ? (stressOutcome === &quot;PASS&quot; ? &quot;STRESS TEST PASS&quot; : stressOutcome === &quot;PARTIAL&quot; ? &quot;STRESS TEST PARTIAL&quot; : &quot;STRESS TEST INCOMPLETE&quot;)
    : structural.errors.length &gt; 0
      ? &quot;INVALID&quot;
      : mathematical.falseNumeric &gt; 0 || mathematical.falseComplex &gt; 0 || mathematical.logical.failures &gt; 0 || mathematical.algebraic.failures &gt; 0
        ? &quot;REVIEW REQUIRED&quot;
        : mathematicalReviewRequired
          ? &quot;MATHEMATICAL REVIEW&quot;
          : status === &quot;VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; ? &quot;ADEC VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; : &quot;ADEC VALID&quot;;

  return {
    title: data.title || &quot;Teoria senza titolo&quot;,
    version: data.version || &quot;n/d&quot;,
    structural, semantic, formal, provenance, mathematical, self, meta,
    score, grade,
    frameworkScore,
    validationScore,
    validationGrade,
    engineIntegrityScore,
    engineIntegrityGrade,
    errors: structural.errors, warnings, infos, status, assessment,
    isStressTest,
    stressCoverage,
    stressPassed,
    stressTotal,
    stressTestCoverage,
    stressTestVerdict,
    stressOracle,
    stressOutcome,
    assessmentReason: isStressTest
      ? (stressOracle &amp;&amp; stressOracle.hasCodeBasedExpectations
          ? &quot;Modalità stress test con oracolo per codici: il verdetto richiede che OGNI codice di finding dichiarato in stress_test.expected sia stato effettivamente rilevato, non solo che la layer si sia attivata.&quot;
          : &quot;Modalità stress test: i difetti deliberatamente inseriti sono stimoli di prova. I punteggi delle singole layer descrivono ciò che il motore ha rilevato; il verdetto dello stress test misura invece se tutte le layer target sono state effettivamente esercitate.&quot;)
      : mathematicalReviewRequired
        ? &quot;La validazione ADEC del framework è distinta dalla coerenza del contenuto analizzato. Sono presenti risultati matematici, algebrici o logici che richiedono revisione.&quot;
        : &quot;Nessuna anomalia matematica/logica automatica rilevata nei passaggi analizzati.&quot;
  };
}

/* =========================================================
   METRICS
   ========================================================= */

function renderMetrics(
  result
) {

  $(&quot;metricScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.score;

  $(&quot;metricGradeBadge&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.grade}`;

  $(&quot;metricNodes&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.structural.nodes.length;

  $(&quot;metricEdges&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.structural.edges.length;

  $(&quot;metricFoundation&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.structural.foundationCoverage}%`;


  $(&quot;resultTheoryTitle&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.title;

  const loadedTheoryBox =
    $(&quot;loadedTheoryName&quot;);

  const loadedTheoryValue =
    $(&quot;loadedTheoryNameValue&quot;);

  if (loadedTheoryBox &amp;&amp; loadedTheoryValue) {
    loadedTheoryValue.textContent =
      result.title || &quot;Teoria senza titolo&quot;;
    loadedTheoryBox.style.display = &quot;&quot;;
  }

  $(&quot;resultTheoryVersion&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `Versione ${result.version}`;

  $(&quot;theoryTitleBadge&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.title;

  $(&quot;theoryTitleBadge&quot;)
    .classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);


  /* Layer cards */

  $(&quot;layerStructuralScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.structural.score;

  $(&quot;layerStructuralGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.structural.grade}`;

  $(&quot;layerStructuralChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.structural.nodes.length} nodi · ${result.structural.edges.length} relazioni`;


  $(&quot;layerSemanticScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.semantic.score;

  $(&quot;layerSemanticGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.semantic.grade}`;

  $(&quot;layerSemanticChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.semantic.passed}/${result.semantic.total} controlli superati`;


  $(&quot;layerFormalScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.formal.score;

  $(&quot;layerFormalGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.formal.grade}`;

  $(&quot;layerFormalChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.formal.passed}/${result.formal.total} controlli superati`;


  $(&quot;metricScore&quot;).textContent = result.frameworkScore;
  $(&quot;metricGradeBadge&quot;).textContent = `ADEC ${result.grade}`;

  $(&quot;metricValidationScore&quot;).textContent = result.validationScore;
  $(&quot;metricValidationGrade&quot;).textContent = `GRADE ${result.validationGrade}`;

  $(&quot;metricEngineIntegrity&quot;).textContent = result.engineIntegrityScore;
  $(&quot;metricEngineIntegrityGrade&quot;).textContent = `GRADE ${result.engineIntegrityGrade}`;

  $(&quot;layerMathScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.mathematical.score;
  $(&quot;layerMathGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.mathematical.grade}`;
  $(&quot;layerMathChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.mathematical.checked} step · ${result.mathematical.falseNumeric + result.mathematical.falseComplex} false · ${result.mathematical.review} review`;

  $(&quot;layerAlgebraicScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.mathematical.algebraic.score;
  $(&quot;layerAlgebraicGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.mathematical.algebraic.failures &gt; 0
      ? &quot;ALGEBRA FAIL&quot;
      : result.mathematical.algebraic.review &gt; 0
        ? &quot;REVIEW REQUIRED&quot;
        : `GRADE ${result.mathematical.algebraic.grade}`;
  $(&quot;layerAlgebraicChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${result.mathematical.algebraic.checked} eq · ${result.mathematical.algebraic.verified} identità · ${result.mathematical.algebraic.transformed} trasformazioni · ${result.mathematical.algebraic.review} review`;


  $(&quot;layerSelfScore&quot;)
    .textContent =
    result.self.score;

  $(&quot;layerSelfGrade&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `GRADE ${result.self.grade}`;

  $(&quot;layerSelfChecks&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `Self ${result.self.score} · Meta ${result.meta.score}`;


  const badge =
    $(&quot;globalStatusBadge&quot;);

  badge.textContent =
    result.assessment;


  badge.className =
    &quot;shrink-0 rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold&quot;;


  if (
    result.assessment === &quot;ADEC VALID&quot; || result.assessment === &quot;STRESS TEST PASS&quot;
  ) {

    badge.classList.add(
      &quot;border-emerald-500/30&quot;,
      &quot;bg-emerald-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-emerald-400&quot;
    );

  } else if (
    result.assessment === &quot;ADEC VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; ||
    result.assessment === &quot;MATHEMATICAL REVIEW&quot; ||
    result.assessment === &quot;STRESS TEST INCOMPLETE&quot; ||
    result.assessment === &quot;STRESS TEST PARTIAL&quot;
  ) {

    badge.classList.add(
      &quot;border-amber-500/30&quot;,
      &quot;bg-amber-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-amber-400&quot;
    );

  } else {

    badge.classList.add(
      &quot;border-red-500/30&quot;,
      &quot;bg-red-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-red-400&quot;
    );

  }


  const gradeBadge =
    $(&quot;metricGradeBadge&quot;);

  gradeBadge.className =
    &quot;mt-2 inline-flex rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold&quot;;

  if (result.score &gt;= 90) {

    gradeBadge.classList.add(
      &quot;bg-emerald-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-emerald-400&quot;
    );

  } else if (result.score &gt;= 70) {

    gradeBadge.classList.add(
      &quot;bg-amber-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-amber-400&quot;
    );

  } else {

    gradeBadge.classList.add(
      &quot;bg-red-500/10&quot;,
      &quot;text-red-400&quot;
    );

  }

}
