function createSvgElement(
  tag,
  attrs = {}
) {

  const el =
    document.createElementNS(
      &quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;,
      tag
    );

  for (
    const [key, value]
    of Object.entries(attrs)
  ) {

    el.setAttribute(
      key,
      value
    );

  }

  return el;

}


function calculateGraphLayout(
  result
) {

  const nodes =
    result.structural.nodes;

  const adjacency =
    result.structural.adjacency;

  const positions = new Map();

  if (!nodes.length) {

    return {
      positions,
      width: 1000,
      height: 700
    };

  }


  /*
   * Calculate levels from dependency depth.
   */

  const levels = new Map();

  for (const node of nodes) {

    const deps =
      adjacency.get(node.id) || [];

    let level = 0;

    const queue = [
      {
        id: node.id,
        depth: 0
      }
    ];

    const visited =
      new Set();

    while (queue.length) {

      const current =
        queue.shift();

      if (
        visited.has(current.id)
      )
        continue;

      visited.add(current.id);

      level =
        Math.max(
          level,
          current.depth
        );

      for (
        const dep
        of adjacency.get(current.id) || []
      ) {

        queue.push({
          id: dep,
          depth: current.depth + 1
        });

      }

    }

    levels.set(
      node.id,
      level
    );

  }


  if (
    currentLayout === &quot;horizontal&quot;
  ) {

    const maxLevel =
      Math.max(
        ...levels.values()
      );


    const groups = new Map();

    for (const node of nodes) {

      const level =
        levels.get(node.id) || 0;

      if (!groups.has(level))
        groups.set(level, []);

      groups.get(level).push(node);

    }


    for (
      const [level, group]
      of groups
    ) {

      group.forEach(
        (node, index) =&gt; {

          positions.set(
            node.id,
            {
              x:
                150 +
                level * 260,

              y:
                120 +
                index * 125
            }
          );

        }
      );

    }

    return {
      positions,
      width:
        Math.max(
          1000,
          300 +
          maxLevel * 260
        ),
      height:
        Math.max(
          700,
          Math.max(
            ...Array.from(
              positions.values()
            ).map(
              p =&gt; p.y
            ),
            600
          ) + 150
        )
    };

  }


  /*
   * Hierarchical layout:
   * roots on top, dependent nodes below.
   */

  const maxLevel =
    Math.max(
      ...levels.values()
    );


  const groups = new Map();

  for (const node of nodes) {

    const level =
      levels.get(node.id) || 0;

    if (!groups.has(level))
      groups.set(level, []);

    groups.get(level).push(node);

  }


  /*
   * Keep the node boxes unchanged (170x70), but give each box
   * enough horizontal and vertical breathing room so that the
   * hierarchical view never overlaps or clips nodes.
   */
  const nodeWidth = 170;
  const nodeHeight = 70;
  const horizontalGap = 60;
  const verticalGap = 100;
  const horizontalStep =
    nodeWidth + horizontalGap;
  const verticalStep =
    nodeHeight + verticalGap;

  let maxGroupWidth = nodeWidth;

  for (
    const group
    of groups.values()
  ) {

    maxGroupWidth =
      Math.max(
        maxGroupWidth,
        group.length * nodeWidth +
        Math.max(0, group.length - 1) * horizontalGap
      );

  }

  const canvasWidth =
    Math.max(
      1000,
      maxGroupWidth + 300
    );

  const centerX =
    canvasWidth / 2;

  for (
    const [level, group]
    of groups
  ) {

    const groupWidth =
      group.length * nodeWidth +
      Math.max(0, group.length - 1) * horizontalGap;

    const startX =
      centerX - groupWidth / 2 + nodeWidth / 2;

    group.forEach(
      (node, index) =&gt; {

        positions.set(
          node.id,
          {
            x:
              startX +
              index * horizontalStep,

            y:
              100 +
              level * verticalStep
          }
        );

      }
    );

  }


  return {
    positions,
    width: canvasWidth,
    height:
      Math.max(
        700,
        200 +
        maxLevel * verticalStep
      )
  };

}


function renderGraph(
  result
) {

  const svg =
    $(&quot;graphSvg&quot;);

  const placeholder =
    $(&quot;graphPlaceholder&quot;);

  svg.innerHTML = &quot;&quot;;

  selectedNode = null;

  $(&quot;nodeDetailPanel&quot;)
    .classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);


  if (
    !result ||
    !result.structural.nodes.length
  ) {

    placeholder.classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);

    return;

  }


  placeholder.classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);


  const layout =
    calculateGraphLayout(
      result
    );

  /*
   * v0.8.1 — proof coverage badge lookup.
   * Precomputed once per render so each node only needs an O(1) Set
   * lookup instead of scanning the coverage arrays per node.
   */
  const externalProofIds =
    new Set(
      (result.formal.externalProofNodes || []).map(n =&gt; n.id)
    );

  const evidenceProofIds =
    new Set(
      (result.formal.proofEvidenceNodes || []).map(n =&gt; n.id)
    );

  const proofBearingIds =
    new Set(
      (result.formal.proofBearingNodes || []).map(n =&gt; n.id)
    );


  svg.setAttribute(
    &quot;width&quot;,
    layout.width
  );

  svg.setAttribute(
    &quot;height&quot;,
    layout.height
  );

  svg.setAttribute(
    &quot;viewBox&quot;,
    `0 0 ${layout.width} ${layout.height}`
  );


  /*
   * Definitions.
   */

  const defs =
    createSvgElement(&quot;defs&quot;);


  const marker =
    createSvgElement(
      &quot;marker&quot;,
      {
        id: &quot;arrow&quot;,
        markerWidth: &quot;8&quot;,
        markerHeight: &quot;8&quot;,
        refX: &quot;7&quot;,
        refY: &quot;3&quot;,
        orient: &quot;auto&quot;,
        markerUnits: &quot;strokeWidth&quot;
      }
    );


  const path =
    createSvgElement(
      &quot;path&quot;,
      {
        d: &quot;M0,0 L0,6 L7,3 z&quot;,
        fill: &quot;#64748b&quot;
      }
    );


  marker.appendChild(path);
  defs.appendChild(marker);
  svg.appendChild(defs);


  /*
   * Edges.
   */

  const edgeGroup =
    createSvgElement(&quot;g&quot;);


  for (
    const edge
    of result.structural.edges
  ) {

    if (edge.invalid)
      continue;

    const from =
      layout.positions.get(
        edge.from
      );

    const to =
      layout.positions.get(
        edge.to
      );

    if (!from || !to)
      continue;


    const nodeWidth = 170;
    const nodeHeight = 70;


    let x1 = from.x;
    let y1 = from.y;
    let x2 = to.x;
    let y2 = to.y;


    if (
      currentLayout === &quot;horizontal&quot;
    ) {

      x1 += nodeWidth / 2;
      y1 += nodeHeight / 2;

      x2 -= nodeWidth / 2;
      y2 -= nodeHeight / 2;

    } else {

      x1 = from.x;
      y1 = from.y + nodeHeight / 2;

      x2 = to.x;
      y2 = to.y - nodeHeight / 2;

    }


    const pathD =
      currentLayout === &quot;horizontal&quot;
        ? `M ${x1} ${y1}
           C ${(x1+x2)/2} ${y1},
             ${(x1+x2)/2} ${y2},
             ${x2} ${y2}`
        : `M ${x1} ${y1}
           C ${x1} ${(y1+y2)/2},
             ${x2} ${(y1+y2)/2},
             ${x2} ${y2}`;


    const edgePath =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;path&quot;,
        {
          d:
            pathD
              .replace(/\s+/g, &quot; &quot;)
              .trim(),

          fill: &quot;none&quot;,

          stroke:
            edge.cycle
              ? &quot;#ef4444&quot;
              : &quot;#475569&quot;,

          &quot;stroke-width&quot;:
            edge.cycle
              ? &quot;3&quot;
              : &quot;1.7&quot;,

          &quot;marker-end&quot;:
            &quot;url(#arrow)&quot;,

          &quot;stroke-dasharray&quot;:
            edge.cycle
              ? &quot;7 5&quot;
              : &quot;none&quot;
        }
      );


    edgeGroup.appendChild(
      edgePath
    );

  }


  svg.appendChild(
    edgeGroup
  );


  /*
   * Nodes.
   */

  const nodeGroup =
    createSvgElement(&quot;g&quot;);


  for (
    const node
    of result.structural.nodes
  ) {

    const pos =
      layout.positions.get(
        node.id
      );

    if (!pos)
      continue;


    const style =
      KIND_STYLE[node.kind]
      || KIND_STYLE.concept;


    const group =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;g&quot;,
        {
          class:
            &quot;graph-node&quot;,
          &quot;data-node-id&quot;:
            node.id
        }
      );


    const rect =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;rect&quot;,
        {
          x:
            pos.x - 85,

          y:
            pos.y - 35,

          width:
            170,

          height:
            70,

          rx:
            12,

          fill:
            style.fill,

          stroke:
            style.stroke,

          &quot;stroke-width&quot;:
            1.8
        }
      );


    const idText =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;text&quot;,
        {
          x:
            pos.x,

          y:
            pos.y - 9,

          &quot;text-anchor&quot;:
            &quot;middle&quot;,

          fill:
            style.label,

          &quot;font-size&quot;:
            &quot;11&quot;,

          &quot;font-family&quot;:
            &quot;monospace&quot;,

          &quot;font-weight&quot;:
            &quot;700&quot;
        }
      );


    idText.textContent =
      node.id;


    const nameText =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;text&quot;,
        {
          x:
            pos.x,

          y:
            pos.y + 12,

          &quot;text-anchor&quot;:
            &quot;middle&quot;,

          fill:
            &quot;#e2e8f0&quot;,

          &quot;font-size&quot;:
            &quot;10&quot;,

          &quot;font-family&quot;:
            &quot;Inter, sans-serif&quot;
        }
      );


    let shortName =
      String(
        node.name ||
        node.id
      );


    if (
      shortName.length &gt; 24
    ) {

      shortName =
        shortName.slice(0, 22) +
        &quot;…&quot;;

    }


    nameText.textContent =
      shortName;


    const kindText =
      createSvgElement(
        &quot;text&quot;,
        {
          x:
            pos.x,

          y:
            pos.y + 27,

          &quot;text-anchor&quot;:
            &quot;middle&quot;,

          fill:
            &quot;#64748b&quot;,

          &quot;font-size&quot;:
            &quot;8&quot;,

          &quot;font-family&quot;:
            &quot;monospace&quot;
        }
      );


    kindText.textContent =
      String(
        node.kind || &quot;concept&quot;
      ).toUpperCase();


    group.appendChild(rect);
    group.appendChild(idText);
    group.appendChild(nameText);
    group.appendChild(kindText);


    /*
     * v0.8.1 — proof coverage badge: un piccolo indicatore nell&#39;angolo
     * in alto a destra del nodo, distinto dal colore per &quot;kind&quot; (che
     * resta invariato). Compare solo per i kind proof-bearing:
     *   verde   = prova formale esterna (system+statement+steps+verified)
     *   ciano   = evidenza di validazione ADEC dichiarata (method+steps+status)
     *   ambra   = kind proof-bearing ma senza alcun record proof/evidence
     * I kind non proof-bearing (foundation, definition, constant, ecc.)
     * non ricevono badge.
     */
    if (proofBearingIds.has(node.id)) {

      const badgeColor =
        externalProofIds.has(node.id)
          ? &quot;#34d399&quot;
          : evidenceProofIds.has(node.id)
            ? &quot;#38bdf8&quot;
            : &quot;#f59e0b&quot;;

      const badgeTitleText =
        externalProofIds.has(node.id)
          ? &quot;Prova formale esterna dichiarata&quot;
          : evidenceProofIds.has(node.id)
            ? &quot;Evidenza di validazione ADEC dichiarata&quot;
            : &quot;Nessun record proof/evidence dichiarato&quot;;

      const badge =
        createSvgElement(
          &quot;circle&quot;,
          {
            cx: pos.x + 76,
            cy: pos.y - 27,
            r: 5,
            fill: badgeColor,
            stroke: &quot;#020617&quot;,
            &quot;stroke-width&quot;: 1.2
          }
        );

      const badgeTitle =
        createSvgElement(&quot;title&quot;, {});

      badgeTitle.textContent =
        badgeTitleText;

      badge.appendChild(badgeTitle);

      group.appendChild(badge);

    }


    group.addEventListener(
      &quot;click&quot;,
      event =&gt; {

        event.stopPropagation();

        selectGraphNode(
          node,
          group
        );

      }
    );


    nodeGroup.appendChild(
      group
    );

  }


  svg.appendChild(
    nodeGroup
  );


  renderLegend();

  resetView();

}


/* =========================================================
   GRAPH NODE DETAIL
   ========================================================= */

function selectGraphNode(
  node,
  element
) {

  document
    .querySelectorAll(&quot;.graph-node.selected&quot;)
    .forEach(
      el =&gt;
        el.classList.remove(&quot;selected&quot;)
    );


  element.classList.add(
    &quot;selected&quot;
  );


  selectedNode =
    node;


  $(&quot;nodeDetailPanel&quot;)
    .classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);


  $(&quot;nodeDetailId&quot;)
    .textContent =
    node.id;


  $(&quot;nodeDetailName&quot;)
    .textContent =
    node.name ||
    &quot;(senza nome)&quot;;


  $(&quot;nodeDetailKind&quot;)
    .textContent =
    node.kind ||
    &quot;concept&quot;;


  $(&quot;nodeDetailKind&quot;).className =
    &quot;rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase &quot; +
    (
      node.kind === &quot;theorem&quot;
        ? &quot;bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400&quot;
        : node.kind === &quot;axiom&quot;
          ? &quot;bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400&quot;
          : &quot;bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400&quot;
    );


  $(&quot;nodeDetailDesc&quot;)
    .textContent =
    node.description ||
    &quot;Nessuna descrizione.&quot;;


  const depsContainer =
    $(&quot;nodeDetailDeps&quot;);

  depsContainer.innerHTML = &quot;&quot;;


  const deps =
    Array.isArray(node.deps)
      ? node.deps
      : [];


  if (!deps.length) {

    depsContainer.innerHTML =
      `&lt;span class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-600&quot;&gt;
        Nessuna dipendenza dichiarata.
      &lt;/span&gt;`;

  } else {

    deps.forEach(
      dep =&gt; {

        const tag =
          document.createElement(&quot;span&quot;);

        tag.className =
          &quot;rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-slate-400 code-font&quot;;

        tag.textContent =
          dep;

        depsContainer.appendChild(
          tag
        );

      }
    );

  }

}


/* =========================================================
   GRAPH LEGEND
   ========================================================= */

function renderLegend() {

  const legend =
    $(&quot;graphLegend&quot;);

  legend.innerHTML = &quot;&quot;;


  const kinds = [
    [&quot;foundation&quot;, &quot;Foundation&quot;],
    [&quot;definition&quot;, &quot;Definition&quot;],
    [&quot;axiom&quot;, &quot;Axiom&quot;],
    [&quot;proposition&quot;, &quot;Proposition&quot;],
    [&quot;lemma&quot;, &quot;Lemma&quot;],
    [&quot;theorem&quot;, &quot;Theorem&quot;]
  ];


  kinds.forEach(
    ([kind, label]) =&gt; {

      const style =
        KIND_STYLE[kind];


      const item =
        document.createElement(&quot;span&quot;);

      item.className =
        &quot;flex items-center gap-1&quot;;


      item.innerHTML =
        `&lt;span
          class=&quot;h-2 w-2 rounded-sm&quot;
          style=&quot;
            background:${style.fill};
            border:1px solid ${style.stroke};
          &quot;
        &gt;&lt;/span&gt;
        ${label}`;


      legend.appendChild(
        item
      );

    }
  );


  /*
   * v0.8.1 — legenda del badge di copertura prova (vedi renderGraph).
   */
  const coverageDivider =
    document.createElement(&quot;span&quot;);

  coverageDivider.className =
    &quot;mx-1 text-slate-700&quot;;

  coverageDivider.textContent =
    &quot;·&quot;;

  legend.appendChild(coverageDivider);

  const coverageDots = [
    [&quot;#34d399&quot;, &quot;Prova formale&quot;],
    [&quot;#38bdf8&quot;, &quot;Evidenza ADEC&quot;],
    [&quot;#f59e0b&quot;, &quot;Senza prova&quot;]
  ];

  coverageDots.forEach(
    ([color, label]) =&gt; {

      const item =
        document.createElement(&quot;span&quot;);

      item.className =
        &quot;flex items-center gap-1&quot;;

      item.innerHTML =
        `&lt;span
          class=&quot;h-2 w-2 rounded-full&quot;
          style=&quot;background:${color};&quot;
        &gt;&lt;/span&gt;
        ${label}`;

      legend.appendChild(item);

    }
  );

}


/* =========================================================
   GRAPH VIEW
   ========================================================= */

function applyView() {

  const svg =
    $(&quot;graphSvg&quot;);

  svg.style.transform =
    `translate(${view.x}px, ${view.y}px)
     scale(${view.scale})`;

  $(&quot;zoomLabel&quot;)
    .textContent =
    `${Math.round(view.scale * 100)}%`;

}


function setZoom(
  scale,
  centerX = null,
  centerY = null
) {

  const container =
    $(&quot;graphContainer&quot;);

  const oldScale =
    view.scale;


  const nextScale =
    Math.max(
      0.08,
      Math.min(
        4,
        scale
      )
    );


  if (
    centerX !== null &amp;&amp;
    centerY !== null
  ) {

    const graphX =
      (centerX - view.x) /
      oldScale;

    const graphY =
      (centerY - view.y) /
      oldScale;


    view.x =
      centerX -
      graphX * nextScale;

    view.y =
      centerY -
      graphY * nextScale;

  }


  view.scale =
    nextScale;

  applyView();

}


function zoomIn() {

  const rect =
    $(&quot;graphContainer&quot;)
      .getBoundingClientRect();

  setZoom(
    view.scale * 1.2,
    rect.width / 2,
    rect.height / 2
  );

}


function zoomOut() {

  const rect =
    $(&quot;graphContainer&quot;)
      .getBoundingClientRect();

  setZoom(
    view.scale / 1.2,
    rect.width / 2,
    rect.height / 2
  );

}


function resetView() {

  view.scale = 1;
  view.x = 20;
  view.y = 20;

  applyView();

}


function fitGraph() {

  const container =
    $(&quot;graphContainer&quot;);

  const svg =
    $(&quot;graphSvg&quot;);


  if (
    !svg ||
    !currentResult ||
    !currentResult.structural.nodes.length
  ) {

    resetView();

    return;

  }


  const width =
    Number(
      svg.getAttribute(&quot;width&quot;)
    );

  const height =
    Number(
      svg.getAttribute(&quot;height&quot;)
    );


  const rect =
    container.getBoundingClientRect();


  const padding = 45;


  const scaleX =
    (rect.width - padding * 2) /
    width;

  const scaleY =
    (rect.height - padding * 2) /
    height;


  let scale =
    Math.min(
      scaleX,
      scaleY
    );


  scale =
    Math.max(
      0.08,
      Math.min(
        2,
        scale
      )
    );


  view.scale =
    scale;


  view.x =
    (rect.width -
      width * scale) / 2;


  view.y =
    (rect.height -
      height * scale) / 2;


  applyView();

}


/* =========================================================
   GRAPH PAN / ZOOM EVENTS
   ========================================================= */

const graphContainer =
  $(&quot;graphContainer&quot;);


graphContainer.addEventListener(
  &quot;pointerdown&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    if (
      event.target.closest(&quot;.graph-node&quot;)
    )
      return;


    dragging = true;

    graphContainer.classList.add(
      &quot;dragging&quot;
    );


    dragStart.x =
      event.clientX;

    dragStart.y =
      event.clientY;

    viewStart.x =
      view.x;

    viewStart.y =
      view.y;


    graphContainer.setPointerCapture(
      event.pointerId
    );

  }
);


graphContainer.addEventListener(
  &quot;pointermove&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    if (!dragging)
      return;


    view.x =
      viewStart.x +
      event.clientX -
      dragStart.x;

    view.y =
      viewStart.y +
      event.clientY -
      dragStart.y;


    applyView();

  }
);


graphContainer.addEventListener(
  &quot;pointerup&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    dragging = false;

    graphContainer.classList.remove(
      &quot;dragging&quot;
    );

    try {
      graphContainer.releasePointerCapture(
        event.pointerId
      );
    } catch (_) {}

  }
);


graphContainer.addEventListener(
  &quot;pointercancel&quot;,
  () =&gt; {

    dragging = false;

    graphContainer.classList.remove(
      &quot;dragging&quot;
    );

  }
);


graphContainer.addEventListener(
  &quot;wheel&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    event.preventDefault();


    const rect =
      graphContainer.getBoundingClientRect();


    const x =
      event.clientX -
      rect.left;

    const y =
      event.clientY -
      rect.top;


    const factor =
      event.deltaY &lt; 0
        ? 1.12
        : 0.89;


    setZoom(
      view.scale * factor,
      x,
      y
    );

  },
  {
    passive: false
  }
);


/* =========================================================
   EXPORT GRAPH AS PNG
   ========================================================= */

function exportGraphPNG() {

  if (
    !currentResult ||
    !currentResult.structural ||
    !currentResult.structural.nodes ||
    !currentResult.structural.nodes.length
  ) {

    showToast(
      &quot;Nessun grafo da esportare.&quot;,
      &quot;warning&quot;
    );

    return;

  }

  const sourceSvg =
    $(&quot;graphSvg&quot;);

  if (!sourceSvg) {

    showToast(
      &quot;Grafo SVG non disponibile.&quot;,
      &quot;error&quot;
    );

    return;

  }

  const viewBox =
    sourceSvg.viewBox &amp;&amp;
    sourceSvg.viewBox.baseVal;

  const width =
    Math.max(
      1,
      Number(
        viewBox &amp;&amp; viewBox.width
      ) ||
      Number(
        sourceSvg.getAttribute(&quot;width&quot;)
      ) ||
      1000
    );

  const height =
    Math.max(
      1,
      Number(
        viewBox &amp;&amp; viewBox.height
      ) ||
      Number(
        sourceSvg.getAttribute(&quot;height&quot;)
      ) ||
      700
    );

  const margin = 80;
  const scale = 2;

  const canvas =
    document.createElement(&quot;canvas&quot;);

  canvas.width =
    Math.ceil(
      (width + margin * 2) * scale
    );

  canvas.height =
    Math.ceil(
      (height + margin * 2) * scale
    );

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext(&quot;2d&quot;);

  ctx.scale(
    scale,
    scale
  );

  ctx.fillStyle =
    &quot;#020617&quot;;

  ctx.fillRect(
    0,
    0,
    width + margin * 2,
    height + margin * 2
  );

  ctx.fillStyle =
    &quot;#f8fafc&quot;;

  ctx.font =
    &quot;bold 22px Arial, sans-serif&quot;;

  ctx.fillText(
    currentResult.title || &quot;ADEC Theory&quot;,
    margin,
    35
  );

  ctx.fillStyle =
    &quot;#64748b&quot;;

  ctx.font =
    &quot;12px monospace&quot;;

  ctx.fillText(
    &quot;ADEC Theory Validator v0.8.6&quot;,
    margin,
    57
  );

  const clone =
    sourceSvg.cloneNode(true);

  clone.setAttribute(
    &quot;xmlns&quot;,
    &quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;
  );

  clone.setAttribute(
    &quot;xmlns:xlink&quot;,
    &quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink&quot;
  );

  clone.setAttribute(
    &quot;viewBox&quot;,
    `0 0 ${width} ${height}`
  );

  clone.setAttribute(
    &quot;width&quot;,
    width
  );

  clone.setAttribute(
    &quot;height&quot;,
    height
  );

  const background =
    document.createElementNS(
      &quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;,
      &quot;rect&quot;
    );

  background.setAttribute(
    &quot;x&quot;,
    &quot;0&quot;
  );

  background.setAttribute(
    &quot;y&quot;,
    &quot;0&quot;
  );

  background.setAttribute(
    &quot;width&quot;,
    width
  );

  background.setAttribute(
    &quot;height&quot;,
    height
  );

  background.setAttribute(
    &quot;fill&quot;,
    &quot;#020617&quot;
  );

  clone.insertBefore(
    background,
    clone.firstChild
  );

  const serializer =
    new XMLSerializer();

  const svgString =
    serializer.serializeToString(
      clone
    );

  const blob =
    new Blob(
      [svgString],
      {
        type:
          &quot;image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8&quot;
      }
    );

  const url =
    URL.createObjectURL(
      blob
    );

  const image =
    new Image();

  image.onload = () =&gt; {

    const draw =
      () =&gt; {

        ctx.drawImage(
          image,
          margin,
          75,
          width,
          height
        );

        ctx.fillStyle =
          &quot;#64748b&quot;;

        ctx.font =
          &quot;11px monospace&quot;;

        ctx.fillText(
          `Structural ${currentResult.structural.score} (${currentResult.structural.grade}) · ` +
          `Semantic ${currentResult.semantic.score} (${currentResult.semantic.grade}) · ` +
          `Formal ${currentResult.formal.score} (${currentResult.formal.grade}) · ` +
          `Math ${currentResult.mathematical.score} (${currentResult.mathematical.grade}) · ` +
          `Algebra ${currentResult.mathematical.algebraic.score} (${currentResult.mathematical.algebraic.grade}) · ` +
          `Logic ${currentResult.mathematical.logical.score} (${currentResult.mathematical.logical.grade}) · ` +
          `Framework ${currentResult.frameworkScore} (${currentResult.grade}) · ` +
          `Content ${currentResult.validationScore} (${currentResult.validationGrade})`,
          margin,
          height + 105
        );

        canvas.toBlob(
          pngBlob =&gt; {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
              url
            );

            if (!pngBlob) {

              showToast(
                &quot;Impossibile generare il PNG.&quot;,
                &quot;error&quot;
              );

              return;

            }

            const downloadUrl =
              URL.createObjectURL(
                pngBlob
              );

            const link =
              document.createElement(&quot;a&quot;);

            const safeTitle =
              String(
                currentResult.title ||
                &quot;ADEC_graph&quot;
              )
                .replace(
                  /[^a-z0-9àèéìòù_-]+/gi,
                  &quot;_&quot;
                )
                .replace(
                  /^_+|_+$/g,
                  &quot;&quot;
                );

            link.href =
              downloadUrl;

            link.download =
              `${safeTitle || &quot;ADEC_graph&quot;}_graph.png`;

            link.style.display =
              &quot;none&quot;;

            document.body.appendChild(
              link
            );

            link.click();

            setTimeout(
              () =&gt; {

                link.remove();

                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                  downloadUrl
                );

              },
              1500
            );

            showToast(
              &quot;Grafo completo esportato in PNG.&quot;,
              &quot;success&quot;
            );

          },
          &quot;image/png&quot;
        );

      };

    if (
      typeof image.decode === &quot;function&quot;
    ) {

      image.decode()
        .then(draw)
        .catch(draw);

    } else {

      draw();

    }

  };

  image.onerror = () =&gt; {

    URL.revokeObjectURL(
      url
    );

    showToast(
      &quot;Errore durante la conversione del grafo in PNG.&quot;,
      &quot;error&quot;
    );

  };

  image.src =
    url;

}

/* =========================================================
   DIAGNOSTIC RENDER
   ========================================================= */

function renderDiagnostics(
  result
) {

  clearLog();


  logLine(
    &quot;INFO&quot;,
    &quot;ADEC Engine v0.8.6 avviato.&quot;
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;INFO&quot;,
    `Teoria: ${result.title}`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;OK&quot;,
    `Structural Validation: score ${result.structural.score} (${result.structural.grade}).`
  );

  logLine(
    &quot;STRUCTURAL&quot;,
    `Nodi: ${result.structural.nodes.length} · Relazioni: ${result.structural.edges.length} · Radici: ${result.structural.roots.length} · Terminali: ${result.structural.leaves.length}.`
  );

  logLine(
    &quot;STRUCTURAL&quot;,
    `Componenti: ${result.structural.components} · Profondità massima: ${result.structural.maxDepth} · Foundation coverage: ${result.structural.foundationCoverage}%.`
  );

  logLine(
    &quot;STRUCTURAL&quot;,
    `Errori: ${result.structural.errors.length} · Warning: ${result.structural.warnings.length} · Cicli: ${result.structural.cycles.length}.`
  );


  for (
    const info
    of result.structural.infos
  ) {

    logLine(
      &quot;INFO&quot;,
      info
    );

  }


  logLine(
    &quot;SEMANTIC&quot;,
    `Semantic Validation: score ${result.semantic.score} (${result.semantic.grade}).`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;SEMANTIC&quot;,
    `${result.semantic.total} controlli semantici eseguiti.`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;SEMANTIC&quot;,
    `${result.semantic.passed} controlli semantici superati.`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;FORMAL&quot;,
    `Formal Validation: score ${result.formal.score} (${result.formal.grade}).`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;FORMAL&quot;,
    `${result.formal.total} controlli formali eseguiti.`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;FORMAL&quot;,
    `${result.formal.passed} controlli formali superati.`
  );

  if (result.formal.proofCoverage) {
    const pc = result.formal.proofCoverage;
    logLine(
      &quot;FORMAL&quot;,
      `Proof coverage: ${pc.withEvidence + pc.withExternalFormalProof}/${pc.total} record proof/evidence disponibili; ${pc.withoutProof.length} non forniti.`
    );
    logLine(
      &quot;FORMAL&quot;,
      `Teoremi con proof/evidence: ${pc.theoremWithProof}/${pc.theoremTotal}; proposizioni con proof/evidence: ${pc.propositionWithProof}/${pc.propositionTotal}.`
    );
  }

  if (result.provenance) {
    const p = result.provenance;
    logLine(
      &quot;PROV&quot;,
      `Provenance Diagnostic (NON SCORATO — non contribuisce a Framework/Content/Engine Integrity): ${p.passed}/${p.total} controlli superati.`
    );
    for (const message of p.messages) {
      logLine(&quot;PROV&quot;, message);
    }
  }

  if (result.mathematical) {
    const m = result.mathematical;
    logLine(
      &quot;MATH&quot;,
      `Mathematical Consistency Analysis: ${m.score}/100 (${m.grade}) · relazioni matematiche analizzate: ${m.checked}.`
    );
    logLine(&quot;MATH&quot;, `Tautologie/equivalenze: ${m.tautologies} · uguaglianze numeriche corrette: ${m.trueNumeric} · false: ${m.falseNumeric} · da rivedere: ${m.review}.`);
    if (m.algebraic) {
      logLine(&quot;ALGEBRA&quot;, `Algebraic Consistency Analysis Layer 1.3: ${m.algebraic.score}/100 (${m.algebraic.grade}) · equazioni analizzate: ${m.algebraic.checked} · identità: ${m.algebraic.verified} · trasformazioni: ${m.algebraic.transformed} · review: ${m.algebraic.review}.`);
      for (const item of m.algebraic.results.filter(x =&gt; [&quot;ALGEBRA_FAIL&quot;, &quot;ALGEBRA_TRANSFORM&quot;, &quot;ALGEBRA_IDENTITY&quot;, &quot;ALGEBRA_VERIFIED&quot;, &quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot;].includes(x.status))) {
        const level = item.status === &quot;ALGEBRA_FAIL&quot; ? &quot;ERROR&quot;
          : item.status === &quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot; ? &quot;INFO&quot;
          : &quot;OK&quot;;
        logLine(level, `${item.nodeId} · step ${item.step}: ${item.message}`);
      }
    }
    for (const item of m.results.filter(x =&gt; [&quot;TAUTOLOGY&quot;, &quot;NUMERIC_FAIL&quot;, &quot;REVIEW&quot;].includes(x.status))) {
      logLine(item.status === &quot;NUMERIC_FAIL&quot; ? &quot;ERROR&quot; : item.status === &quot;TAUTOLOGY&quot; ? &quot;WARN&quot; : &quot;INFO&quot;, `${item.nodeId} · step ${item.step}: ${item.message}`);
    }
    if (m.logical) {
      logLine(&quot;LOGICAL&quot;, `Logical Consistency Analysis: ${m.logical.score}/100 (${m.logical.grade}) · step analizzati: ${m.logical.checkedSteps} · criticità: ${m.logical.failures} · review: ${m.logical.warnings}.`);
      for (const item of m.logical.findings) {
        logLine([&quot;LOGICAL_FAIL&quot;,&quot;LOGICAL_CONTRADICTION&quot;,&quot;LOGICAL_UNSUPPORTED&quot;,&quot;LOGICAL_ASSUMED&quot;,&quot;LOGICAL_GAP&quot;].includes(item.status) ? &quot;ERROR&quot; : &quot;INFO&quot;, `${item.nodeId} · step ${item.step}: ${item.message}`);
      }
    }
  }

  for (const info of (result.infos || [])) {
    logLine(&quot;INFO&quot;, info);
  }


  logLine(
    &quot;SELF&quot;,
    &quot;ADEC Self-Validation del motore avviata.&quot;
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;OK&quot;,
    `Self-Validation engine: score ${result.self.score} (${result.self.grade}).`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;SELF&quot;,
    `${result.self.passed}/${result.self.total} controlli self-validation superati.`
  );


  for (const check of result.self.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed)) {
    logLine(
      &quot;WARN&quot;,
      `Self check fallito: ${check.label}.`
    );

    if (check.detail) {
      logLine(
        &quot;SELF&quot;,
        `Diagnostica: ${check.detail}`
      );
    }
  }

  if (result.self.diagnostics) {

    const sd = result.self.diagnostics;

    logLine(
      &quot;SELF&quot;,
      `Semantic self-model: ${sd.semanticPassed}/${sd.semanticTotal} controlli superati.`
    );

    for (const failure of sd.semanticFailures) {
      logLine(
        &quot;SELF&quot;,
        `Semantic failure detail: ${failure.label}${failure.detail ? ` — ${failure.detail}` : &quot;&quot;}.`
      );
    }

    logLine(
      &quot;SELF&quot;,
      `Formal self-model: ${sd.formalPassed}/${sd.formalTotal} controlli superati.`
    );

    for (const failure of sd.formalFailures) {
      logLine(
        &quot;SELF&quot;,
        `Formal failure detail: ${failure.label}${failure.detail ? ` — ${failure.detail}` : &quot;&quot;}.`
      );
    }

    logLine(
      &quot;SELF&quot;,
      `Proof/evidence self-model: theorem=[${sd.theoremNodes.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}], validation-evidence=[${sd.proofEvidenceNodes.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}], external-formal=[${sd.externalProofNodes.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;nessuno&quot;}].`
    );

  }


  logLine(
    &quot;META&quot;,
    `Meta-Validation: score ${result.meta.score} (${result.meta.grade}).`
  );


  logLine(
    &quot;META&quot;,
    `${result.meta.passed}/${result.meta.total} controlli meta superati.`
  );

  logLine(
    &quot;SELF&quot;,
    `Self-model: ${result.self.modelTitle}.`
  );


  for (
    const error
    of result.errors
  ) {

    logLine(
      &quot;ERROR&quot;,
      error
    );

  }


  for (
    const warning
    of result.warnings
  ) {

    if (
      !result.formal.theoremNodes.some(
        node =&gt;
          warning.includes(
            `&quot;${node.id}&quot;`
          )
      )
    ) {

      logLine(
        &quot;WARN&quot;,
        warning
      );

    }

  }


  if (result.isStressTest) {

    logLine(
      result.stressOutcome === &quot;PASS&quot; ? &quot;OK&quot; : &quot;ERROR&quot;,
      `Stress Test Verdict: ${result.stressTestVerdict}. ` +
      `Layer coverage ${result.stressPassed}/${result.stressTotal} (${result.stressTestCoverage}%).`
    );

    /*
     * v0.8.2 — dettaglio dell&#39;Expected Findings Oracle, categoria per
     * categoria, quando il fixture dichiara stress_test.expected con
     * array di codici. Ricalca lo schema Expected/Detected/Missing/
     * Unexpected richiesto.
     */
    if (result.stressOracle &amp;&amp; result.stressOracle.hasCodeBasedExpectations) {

      const categoryLabels = {
        structural: &quot;Structural&quot;,
        semantic: &quot;Semantic&quot;,
        formal: &quot;Formal&quot;,
        mathematical: &quot;Mathematical&quot;,
        algebraic: &quot;Algebraic&quot;,
        logical: &quot;Logical&quot;,
        provenance: &quot;Provenance (non scorato)&quot;
      };

      for (const [key, label] of Object.entries(categoryLabels)) {

        const cat = result.stressOracle.categories[key];
        if (!cat) continue;

        logLine(
          cat.missing.length === 0 ? &quot;OK&quot; : &quot;ERROR&quot;,
          `Oracle · ${label} — expected [${cat.expected.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;—&quot;}] · ` +
          `detected [${cat.detected.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;—&quot;}] · ` +
          `missing [${cat.missing.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;—&quot;}]` +
          (cat.unexpected.length ? ` · unexpected [${cat.unexpected.join(&quot;, &quot;)}]` : &quot;&quot;)
        );

      }

      if (result.stressOracle.selfMetaExpected) {
        logLine(
          result.stressOracle.selfMetaOk ? &quot;OK&quot; : &quot;ERROR&quot;,
          `Oracle · Self/Meta — atteso integro (self=100, meta=100): ${
            result.stressOracle.selfMetaOk ? &quot;confermato&quot; : &quot;NON confermato&quot;
          }.`
        );
      }

    }

    logLine(
      &quot;INFO&quot;,
      `Stress-test interpretation: Framework ${result.frameworkScore} (${result.grade}) and Content ${result.validationScore} (${result.validationGrade}) are diagnostic scores of the deliberately corrupted fixture, not engine-accuracy scores.`
    );
  }

  logLine(
    result.isStressTest ? &quot;INFO&quot; :
    result.assessment === &quot;REVIEW REQUIRED&quot; ? &quot;ERROR&quot;
      : result.assessment === &quot;MATHEMATICAL REVIEW&quot; ? &quot;WARN&quot;
      : result.status === &quot;VALID&quot; ? &quot;OK&quot; : &quot;WARN&quot;,
    `Global Assessment: ${result.assessment}. ` +
    `ADEC Framework ${result.frameworkScore} (${result.grade}) · ` +
    `Content Validation ${result.validationScore} (${result.validationGrade}) · ` +
    `Engine Integrity ${result.engineIntegrityScore} (${result.engineIntegrityGrade}).`
  );

}


/* =========================================================
   DETAILED VALIDATION WINDOW
   ========================================================= */

/* escapeHtml è già definita in HELPERS (v0.8.1: rimossa la ridefinizione duplicata). */

function renderValidationDetails() {
  if (!currentResult) return;

  const r = currentResult;
  const pc = r.formal.proofCoverage || { total: 0, withEvidence: 0, withExternalFormalProof: 0, withoutProof: [], theoremTotal: 0, theoremWithProof: 0, propositionTotal: 0, propositionWithProof: 0 };
  const math = r.mathematical || null;
  const semanticFailures = r.semantic.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed);
  const formalFailures = r.formal.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed);
  const selfFailures = r.self.checks.filter(c =&gt; !c.passed);

  const layer = (name, score, grade, extra = &quot;&quot;) =&gt; `
    &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;flex items-center justify-between&quot;&gt;
        &lt;span class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(name)}&lt;/span&gt;
        &lt;span class=&quot;text-sm font-bold text-white&quot;&gt;${score} &lt;span class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(grade)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      ${extra}
    &lt;/div&gt;`;

  const list = (items, empty = &quot;Nessun elemento.&quot;) =&gt; items.length ? `&lt;ul class=&quot;mt-2 space-y-1&quot;&gt;${items.map(x =&gt; `&lt;li class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400&quot;&gt;${x}&lt;/li&gt;`).join(&quot;&quot;)}&lt;/ul&gt;` : `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[11px] text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;${empty}&lt;/div&gt;`;

  const proofRows = r.structural.nodes
    .filter(n =&gt; [&quot;proposition&quot;,&quot;lemma&quot;,&quot;theorem&quot;,&quot;corollary&quot;,&quot;self_validation&quot;,&quot;meta_validation&quot;].includes(n.kind))
    .map(n =&gt; {
      const p = n.proof;
      const has = p &amp;&amp; typeof p === &quot;object&quot;;
      const external = has &amp;&amp; typeof p.system === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; typeof p.statement === &quot;string&quot;;
      const evidence = has &amp;&amp; typeof p.method === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(p.steps);
      const status = has ? (p.status || (p.verified === true ? &quot;verified&quot; : &quot;declared&quot;)) : &quot;non fornito&quot;;
      const state = external ? &quot;Prova formale esterna&quot; : evidence ? &quot;Evidence ADEC&quot; : &quot;Non fornito&quot;;
      const stateClass = external || evidence ? &quot;text-emerald-400&quot; : &quot;text-slate-500&quot;;
      const deps = Array.isArray(n.deps) ? n.deps.join(&quot;, &quot;) || &quot;—&quot; : &quot;—&quot;;
      const proofMeta = has
        ? `${p.method || p.system || &quot;record&quot;} · ${Array.isArray(p.steps) ? p.steps.length : 0} step`
        : &quot;Nessun record proof/evidence&quot;;
      return `&lt;tr class=&quot;border-t border-slate-800 align-top&quot;&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 font-mono text-blue-300&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(n.id)}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(n.name)}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(n.kind)}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 ${stateClass}&quot;&gt;${state}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(status)}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(deps)}&lt;/td&gt;
        &lt;td class=&quot;px-3 py-2 text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(proofMeta)}&lt;/td&gt;
      &lt;/tr&gt;`;
    }).join(&quot;&quot;);

  $(&quot;detailModalBody&quot;).innerHTML = `
    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5&quot;&gt;
      ${layer(&quot;Structural&quot;, r.structural.score, r.structural.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.structural.nodes.length} nodi · ${r.structural.edges.length} relazioni · ${r.structural.errors.length} errori · ${r.structural.warnings.length} warning&lt;/div&gt;`)}
      ${layer(&quot;Semantic&quot;, r.semantic.score, r.semantic.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.semantic.passed}/${r.semantic.total} controlli&lt;/div&gt;`)}
      ${layer(&quot;Formal representation&quot;, r.formal.score, r.formal.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.formal.passed}/${r.formal.total} controlli&lt;/div&gt;`)}
      ${layer(&quot;Mathematical consistency&quot;, r.mathematical.score, r.mathematical.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.checked} relazioni · ${r.mathematical.falseNumeric + r.mathematical.falseComplex} false · ${r.mathematical.review} review&lt;/div&gt;`)}
      ${layer(&quot;Self&quot;, r.self.score, r.self.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.self.passed}/${r.self.total} controlli&lt;/div&gt;`)}
      ${layer(&quot;Meta&quot;, r.meta.score, r.meta.grade, `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.meta.passed}/${r.meta.total} controlli&lt;/div&gt;`)}
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/5 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300&quot;&gt;Structural Validation — diagnostica completa&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;p class=&quot;mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400&quot;&gt;Il livello Structural analizza la topologia dichiarata: nodi, relazioni, dipendenze, radici, terminali, componenti, profondità, foundation coverage, duplicazioni, riferimenti inesistenti, auto-dipendenze, cicli e anomalie descrittive.&lt;/p&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Structural Validation Report&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.structural.nodes.length} nodi · ${r.structural.edges.length} relazioni&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5 px-3 py-2 text-right&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Score strutturale&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-lg font-bold text-white&quot;&gt;${r.structural.score} &lt;span class=&quot;text-xs text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(r.structural.grade)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-7&quot;&gt;
        ${[[&quot;Nodi&quot;,r.structural.nodes.length],[&quot;Relazioni&quot;,r.structural.edges.length],[&quot;Radici&quot;,r.structural.roots.length],[&quot;Terminali&quot;,r.structural.leaves.length],[&quot;Componenti&quot;,r.structural.components],[&quot;Profondità max&quot;,r.structural.maxDepth],[&quot;Foundation coverage&quot;,r.structural.foundationCoverage+&quot;%&quot;]].map(([k,v])=&gt;`&lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${k}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-200&quot;&gt;${v}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`).join(&#39;&#39;)}
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-red-500/10 bg-red-500/5 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Errori&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${r.structural.errors.length ? &#39;text-red-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.structural.errors.length}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-amber-500/10 bg-amber-500/5 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Warning&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${r.structural.warnings.length ? &#39;text-amber-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.structural.warnings.length}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Cicli&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${r.structural.cycles.length ? &#39;text-red-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.structural.cycles.length}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Caratteristiche strutturali&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3&quot;&gt;
        ${[
          [&quot;Nodi analizzati&quot;, r.structural.nodes.length],
          [&quot;Relazioni analizzate&quot;, r.structural.edges.length],
          [&quot;Nodi radice&quot;, r.structural.roots.length],
          [&quot;Nodi terminali&quot;, r.structural.leaves.length],
          [&quot;Componenti connesse&quot;, r.structural.components],
          [&quot;Profondità massima&quot;, r.structural.maxDepth],
          [&quot;Foundation coverage&quot;, r.structural.foundationCoverage + &quot;%&quot;],
          [&quot;Cicli rilevati&quot;, r.structural.cycles.length],
          [&quot;Errori strutturali&quot;, r.structural.errors.length],
          [&quot;Warning strutturali&quot;, r.structural.warnings.length],
          [&quot;Dipendenze valide&quot;, r.structural.edges.filter(e =&gt; !e.invalid).length],
          [&quot;Dipendenze non valide&quot;, r.structural.edges.filter(e =&gt; e.invalid).length]
        ].map(([k,v]) =&gt; `&lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(String(k))}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-200&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(String(v))}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`).join(&#39;&#39;)}
      &lt;/div&gt;

      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Controlli strutturali&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
        ${(r.structural.structuralChecks || []).map(c =&gt; `&lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;flex items-start justify-between gap-3&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-400&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(c.label)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;shrink-0 text-[9px] font-bold ${c.passed ? &#39;text-emerald-400&#39; : &#39;text-red-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${c.passed ? &#39;PASS&#39; : &#39;FAIL&#39;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;${c.detail ? `&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[9px] leading-relaxed text-slate-600&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(c.detail)}&lt;/div&gt;` : &#39;&#39;}&lt;/div&gt;`).join(&#39;&#39;)}
      &lt;/div&gt;

      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;
          &lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Caratteristiche della topologia&lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 space-y-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Componenti: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(String(r.structural.components))}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

            &lt;div&gt;Radici: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${r.structural.roots.length ? r.structural.roots.map(n =&gt; escapeHtml(n.id)).join(&#39;, &#39;) : &#39;nessuna&#39;}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Terminali: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${r.structural.leaves.length ? r.structural.leaves.map(n =&gt; escapeHtml(n.id)).join(&#39;, &#39;) : &#39;nessuno&#39;}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;
