          &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 space-y-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Cicli: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${r.structural.cycles.length}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Dipendenze valide: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${r.structural.edges.filter(e =&gt; !e.invalid).length}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Dipendenze non valide: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${r.structural.edges.filter(e =&gt; e.invalid).length}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div&gt;Foundation coverage: &lt;strong class=&quot;text-slate-300&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(String(r.structural.foundationCoverage))}%&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Radici&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1&quot;&gt;${r.structural.roots.length ? r.structural.roots.map(n =&gt; `&lt;span class=&quot;rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-[9px] text-slate-400&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(n.id)}&lt;/span&gt;`).join(&#39;&#39;) : &#39;&lt;span class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Nessuna&lt;/span&gt;&#39;}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Terminali&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1&quot;&gt;${r.structural.leaves.length ? r.structural.leaves.map(n =&gt; `&lt;span class=&quot;rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-[9px] text-slate-400&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(n.id)}&lt;/span&gt;`).join(&#39;&#39;) : &#39;&lt;span class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Nessuno&lt;/span&gt;&#39;}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Errori strutturali&lt;/div&gt;${list((r.structural.errors || []).map(x =&gt; escapeHtml(x)), &#39;Nessun errore strutturale.&#39;)}&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Warning strutturali&lt;/div&gt;${list((r.structural.warnings || []).map(x =&gt; escapeHtml(x)), &#39;Nessun warning strutturale.&#39;)}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Cicli di dipendenza&lt;/div&gt;
        ${list((r.structural.cycles || []).map(c =&gt; escapeHtml(Array.isArray(c) ? c.join(&#39; → &#39;) : String(c))), &#39;Nessun ciclo di dipendenza rilevato.&#39;)}
      &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/5 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300&quot;&gt;ADEC: validazione ≠ proof coverage&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;p class=&quot;mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400&quot;&gt;L&#39;assenza di un record proof/evidence è un&#39;informazione, non un errore. La validazione della rappresentazione e la disponibilità di una dimostrazione sono livelli distinti: una teoria può essere validata strutturalmente, semanticamente e formalmente anche quando le dimostrazioni non sono state fornite.&lt;/p&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;flex items-center justify-between&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Proof Coverage — informativa, non penalizzante&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;text-lg font-bold text-white&quot;&gt;${pc.withEvidence + pc.withExternalFormalProof}/${pc.total}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Evidence ADEC&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-200&quot;&gt;${pc.withEvidence}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Proof formali esterne&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-200&quot;&gt;${pc.withExternalFormalProof}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Non forniti&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-amber-400&quot;&gt;${pc.withoutProof.length}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-violet-500/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-4&quot;&gt;
       &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-violet-300&quot;&gt;Formal Representation ≠ Proof Coverage&lt;/div&gt;
       &lt;p class=&quot;mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400&quot;&gt;
         Il livello Formal verifica la qualità della rappresentazione formale dichiarata.
         Gli status epistemici distinguono tra evidenza verificata, dichiarata, proposta o non verificata.
         Proof Coverage indica soltanto quanta evidenza è stata fornita. L&#39;assenza di
         proof/evidence non implica falsità, errore formale o mancata validazione.
         Una dimostrazione formale esterna richiede un proof checker dedicato.
       &lt;/p&gt;
     &lt;/div&gt;

     &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Dettaglio nodi proof-bearing&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-3 overflow-x-auto&quot;&gt;&lt;table class=&quot;w-full text-left text-[10px]&quot;&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr class=&quot;text-slate-500&quot;&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;ID&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Nome&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Kind&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Copertura&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Status&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Dipendenze&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class=&quot;px-3 py-2&quot;&gt;Record&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;${proofRows || `&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td colspan=&quot;7&quot; class=&quot;px-3 py-3 text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Nessun nodo proof-bearing.&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;`}&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/5 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;flex items-center justify-between gap-3&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div&gt;
          &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300&quot;&gt;Mathematical &amp;amp; Logical Consistency&lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Analisi conservativa dei passaggi espliciti; non sostituisce un theorem prover.&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;text-right&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-lg font-bold text-white&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.score} &lt;span class=&quot;text-xs text-amber-400&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(r.mathematical.grade)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${escapeHtml(r.assessment)}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Relazioni&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-200&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.checked}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;False&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${(r.mathematical.falseNumeric + r.mathematical.falseComplex) ? &#39;text-red-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.falseNumeric + r.mathematical.falseComplex}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Review&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-amber-400&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.review}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Logica critica&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${r.mathematical.logical.failures ? &#39;text-red-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.logical.failures}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Algebra trasformazioni&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? r.mathematical.algebraic.transformed : 0}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 lg:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Anomalie matematiche&lt;/div&gt;${list(r.mathematical.results.filter(x =&gt; x.status === &#39;NUMERIC_FAIL&#39; || x.status === &#39;TAUTOLOGY&#39; || x.status === &#39;REVIEW&#39;).map(x =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(x.nodeId)} · step ${x.step}&lt;/strong&gt; — ${escapeHtml(x.message)}`), &#39;Nessuna anomalia matematica automatica.&#39;)}&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Anomalie logiche&lt;/div&gt;${list(r.mathematical.logical.findings.map(x =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(x.nodeId)} · step ${x.step}&lt;/strong&gt; — ${escapeHtml(x.message)}`), &#39;Nessuna anomalia logica automatica.&#39;)}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-300&quot;&gt;Algebraic Consistency — Layer 1.3&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400&quot;&gt;Theory-independent parser: custom symbols are treated generically; unsupported mathematics remains REVIEW.&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;p class=&quot;mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400&quot;&gt;Verifica conservativa di identità e trasformazioni algebriche tra equazioni. Le trasformazioni che richiedono ipotesi aggiuntive, funzioni non supportate o condizioni di dominio restano in REVIEW.&lt;/p&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? `${r.mathematical.algebraic.transformed} trasformazioni verificate · ${r.mathematical.algebraic.verified} verifiche/identità · ${r.mathematical.algebraic.failures} fallimenti · ${r.mathematical.algebraic.review} review` : &quot;Layer non disponibile&quot;}&lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Diagnostica Algebraic Consistency — Layer 1.3&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Verificate&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? r.mathematical.algebraic.verified : 0}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Trasformazioni&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? r.mathematical.algebraic.transformed : 0}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Fallimenti&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold ${r.mathematical.algebraic &amp;&amp; r.mathematical.algebraic.failures ? &#39;text-red-400&#39; : &#39;text-emerald-400&#39;}&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? r.mathematical.algebraic.failures : 0}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[9px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Review&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-amber-400&quot;&gt;${r.mathematical.algebraic ? r.mathematical.algebraic.review : 0}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      ${list((r.mathematical.algebraic?.results || []).filter(x =&gt; [&quot;ALGEBRA_FAIL&quot;,&quot;ALGEBRA_REVIEW&quot;].includes(x.status)).map(x =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(x.nodeId)} · step ${x.step}&lt;/strong&gt; — ${escapeHtml(x.message)}`), &#39;Nessun fallimento o review algebrica.&#39;) }
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;mb-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Interpretazione dello stato&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;mt-2 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3&quot;&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-400&quot;&gt;PASS&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Controllo superato.&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-400&quot;&gt;INFO&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Dato non fornito o copertura assente, senza violazione.&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-3&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400&quot;&gt;WARN&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Anomalia o record dichiarato ma incompleto/incoerente.&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Semantic — controlli non superati&lt;/div&gt;${list(semanticFailures.map(c =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(c.label)}&lt;/strong&gt;${c.warning ? ` — ${escapeHtml(c.warning)}` : &quot;&quot;}`), &quot;Tutti i controlli superati.&quot;)}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Formal — controlli non superati&lt;/div&gt;${list(formalFailures.map(c =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(c.label)}&lt;/strong&gt;${c.detail ? ` — ${escapeHtml(c.detail)}` : &quot;&quot;}`), &quot;Tutti i controlli superati.&quot;)}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Self — diagnostica&lt;/div&gt;${list(selfFailures.map(c =&gt; `&lt;strong&gt;${escapeHtml(c.label)}&lt;/strong&gt;${c.detail ? ` — ${escapeHtml(c.detail)}` : &quot;&quot;}`), &quot;Tutti i controlli self superati.&quot;)}&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300&quot;&gt;Structural — errori e warning&lt;/div&gt;${list([...(r.structural.errors || []), ...(r.structural.warnings || [])].map(x =&gt; escapeHtml(x)), &quot;Nessun errore o warning strutturale.&quot;)}&lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;`;

  $(&quot;detailModalSubtitle&quot;).textContent = `${r.title} · v${r.version} · Framework ${r.frameworkScore} (${r.grade}) · Content ${r.validationScore} (${r.validationGrade}) · Engine ${r.engineIntegrityScore} (${r.engineIntegrityGrade}) · ${r.assessment}`;
}

function openValidationDetails() {
  const currentText =
    $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
      .value
      .trim();

  if (
    !currentResult ||
    validatedEditorText === null ||
    currentText !== validatedEditorText
  ) {
    showToast(
      &quot;Il JSON è stato modificato. Esegui nuovamente la validazione.&quot;,
      &quot;warning&quot;
    );
    return;
  }
  renderValidationDetails();
  $(&quot;detailModal&quot;).classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);
  $(&quot;detailModal&quot;).classList.add(&quot;flex&quot;);
}

function closeValidationDetails() {
  $(&quot;detailModal&quot;).classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);
  $(&quot;detailModal&quot;).classList.remove(&quot;flex&quot;);
}

/* =========================================================
   VALIDATION EXECUTION
   ========================================================= */

function resetValidationView(reason = &quot;&quot;) {

  currentResult = null;
  currentTheory = null;
  validatedEditorText = null;
  validationRevision++;
  selectedNode = null;

  const graphSvg = $(&quot;graphSvg&quot;);
  if (graphSvg) graphSvg.innerHTML = &quot;&quot;;

  const detailPanel = $(&quot;nodeDetailPanel&quot;);
  if (detailPanel) detailPanel.classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);

  const resetText = {
    metricScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    metricNodes: &quot;--&quot;,
    metricEdges: &quot;--&quot;,
    metricFoundation: &quot;--&quot;,
    metricGradeBadge: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerStructuralScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerStructuralGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerStructuralChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    layerSemanticScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerSemanticGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerSemanticChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    layerFormalScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerFormalGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerFormalChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    layerMathScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerMathGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerMathChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    layerAlgebraicScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerAlgebraicGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerAlgebraicChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    layerSelfScore: &quot;--&quot;,
    layerSelfGrade: &quot;GRADE --&quot;,
    layerSelfChecks: &quot;-- controlli&quot;,
    resultTheoryTitle: &quot;Nessuna teoria validata&quot;,
    resultTheoryVersion: &quot;Versione —&quot;,
    theoryTitleBadge: &quot;&quot;,
    nodeDetailId: &quot;&quot;,
    nodeDetailName: &quot;&quot;,
    nodeDetailKind: &quot;&quot;,
    nodeDetailDesc: &quot;&quot;,
    nodeDetailDeps: &quot;&quot;
  };

  Object.entries(resetText).forEach(([id, value]) =&gt; {
    const el = $(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = value;
  });

  const badge = $(&quot;theoryTitleBadge&quot;);
  if (badge) badge.classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);

  const loaded = $(&quot;loadedTheoryName&quot;);
  if (loaded) loaded.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;

  const global = $(&quot;globalStatusBadge&quot;);
  if (global) {
    global.textContent = reason ? &quot;JSON NON VALIDO&quot; : &quot;IDLE&quot;;
    global.className =
      &quot;shrink-0 rounded-full border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400&quot;;
  }

  clearLog();

  if (reason) {
    logLine(&quot;ERROR&quot;, reason);
  }

  const engine = $(&quot;engineStatus&quot;);
  if (engine) {
    engine.innerHTML = reason
      ? &#39;&lt;span class=&quot;status-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/span&gt; JSON NON VALIDO&#39;
      : &#39;&lt;span class=&quot;status-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/span&gt; MODIFICATO — VALIDAZIONE NECESSARIA&#39;;
  }

}


function runValidation() {

  const text =
    $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
      .value
      .trim();

  // Ogni nuova richiesta di validazione parte da uno stato pulito.
  // In questo modo nessun risultato precedente può sopravvivere a un
  // JSON modificato o a un JSON sintatticamente non valido.
  resetValidationView();


  if (!text) {

    showJsonError(
      &quot;L&#39;editor JSON è vuoto.&quot;
    );

    return;

  }


  let data;


  try {

    data =
      JSON.parse(text);

  } catch (error) {

    const message =
      `JSON non valido: ${error.message}`;

    resetValidationView(message);
    showJsonError(message);

    return;

  }


  clearJsonError();


  if (
    !data ||
    typeof data !== &quot;object&quot;
  ) {

    showJsonError(
      &quot;Il documento JSON deve essere un oggetto.&quot;
    );

    return;

  }


  if (
    !Array.isArray(data.nodes)
  ) {

    showJsonError(
      &#39;Il campo &quot;nodes&quot; deve essere un array.&#39;
    );

    return;

  }


  currentTheory =
    data;

  validatedEditorText =
    text;

  validationRevision++;


  currentResult =
    validateTheory(
      data
    );

  /* Keep the visible loaded-theory label synchronized with the actual
   * validated result. Top-level let bindings are not window properties, so
   * polling window.currentResult is intentionally avoided. */
  if (typeof window.updateAdecLoadedTheoryName === &quot;function&quot;) {
    window.updateAdecLoadedTheoryName(currentResult);
  }
  document.dispatchEvent(
    new CustomEvent(&quot;ADEC:result-loaded&quot;, { detail: currentResult })
  );


  /* v0.8.1 — registra un riepilogo di questa validazione nello storico. */
  validationHistory.push({
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    title: currentResult.title,
    version: currentResult.version,
    frameworkScore: currentResult.frameworkScore,
    grade: currentResult.grade,
    validationScore: currentResult.validationScore,
    validationGrade: currentResult.validationGrade,
    engineIntegrityScore: currentResult.engineIntegrityScore,
    engineIntegrityGrade: currentResult.engineIntegrityGrade,
    status: currentResult.status,
    assessment: currentResult.assessment,
    isStressTest: currentResult.isStressTest
  });

  if (validationHistory.length &gt; VALIDATION_HISTORY_LIMIT) {
    validationHistory =
      validationHistory.slice(
        validationHistory.length - VALIDATION_HISTORY_LIMIT
      );
  }


  renderMetrics(
    currentResult
  );


  renderDiagnostics(
    currentResult
  );


  renderGraph(
    currentResult
  );


  setTimeout(
    fitGraph,
    30
  );


  $(&quot;editorState&quot;)
    .classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);


  $(&quot;engineStatus&quot;)
    .innerHTML =
    `&lt;span
       class=&quot;status-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${
         currentResult.status === &quot;INVALID&quot;
           ? &quot;bg-red-500&quot;
           : (currentResult.status === &quot;VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; ||
              currentResult.status === &quot;STRESS TEST INCOMPLETE&quot; ||
              currentResult.status === &quot;STRESS TEST PARTIAL&quot;)
             ? &quot;bg-amber-500&quot;
             : &quot;bg-emerald-500&quot;
       }&quot;
     &gt;&lt;/span&gt;
     ${currentResult.status}`;


  showToast(
    `Teoria &quot;${currentResult.title}&quot; validata.`,
    currentResult.status === &quot;INVALID&quot;
      ? &quot;error&quot;
      : (currentResult.status === &quot;VALID WITH WARNINGS&quot; ||
         currentResult.status === &quot;STRESS TEST INCOMPLETE&quot; ||
         currentResult.status === &quot;STRESS TEST PARTIAL&quot;)
        ? &quot;warning&quot;
        : &quot;success&quot;
  );

}


/* =========================================================
   JSON ERROR
   ========================================================= */

function showJsonError(
  message
) {

  $(&quot;jsonError&quot;)
    .classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);

  $(&quot;jsonError&quot;)
    .classList.add(&quot;flex&quot;);

  $(&quot;jsonErrorMsg&quot;)
    .textContent =
    message;

}


function clearJsonError() {

  $(&quot;jsonError&quot;)
    .classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);

  $(&quot;jsonError&quot;)
    .classList.remove(&quot;flex&quot;);

}


/* =========================================================
   EDITOR
   ========================================================= */

function updateNodeHint() {

  const text =
    $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
      .value
      .trim();


  if (!text) {

    $(&quot;nodeCountHint&quot;)
      .textContent =
      &quot;0 nodi&quot;;

    return;

  }


  try {

    const data =
      JSON.parse(text);

    const count =
      Array.isArray(data.nodes)
        ? data.nodes.length
        : 0;

    $(&quot;nodeCountHint&quot;)
      .textContent =
      `${count} ${count === 1 ? &quot;nodo&quot; : &quot;nodi&quot;}`;

  } catch (_) {

    $(&quot;nodeCountHint&quot;)
      .textContent =
      &quot;JSON non valido&quot;;

  }

}


$(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
  .addEventListener(
    &quot;input&quot;,
    () =&gt; {

      const currentText =
        $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
          .value
          .trim();

      // Il risultato precedente non può più rappresentare
      // il contenuto corrente dell&#39;editor.
      if (
        validatedEditorText !== null &amp;&amp;
        currentText !== validatedEditorText
      ) {

        resetValidationView();
        clearJsonError();

      }

      $(&quot;editorState&quot;)
        .classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);

      $(&quot;editorState&quot;)
        .textContent =
        &quot;MODIFICATO — VALIDAZIONE NECESSARIA&quot;;

      $(&quot;engineStatus&quot;)
        .innerHTML =
        `&lt;span class=&quot;status-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/span&gt;
         MODIFICATO — VALIDAZIONE NECESSARIA`;

      updateNodeHint();

    }
  );


$(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
  .addEventListener(
    &quot;keydown&quot;,
    event =&gt; {

      if (
        (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &amp;&amp;
        event.key === &quot;Enter&quot;
      ) {

        event.preventDefault();

        runValidation();

      }

    }
  );


/* =========================================================
   JSON DOWNLOAD
   ========================================================= */

function downloadJSON() {

  const text =
    $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
      .value
      .trim();


  if (!text) {

    showToast(
      &quot;Nessun JSON da esportare.&quot;,
      &quot;warning&quot;
    );

    return;

  }


  let data;


  try {

    data =
      JSON.parse(text);

  } catch (error) {

    showToast(
      &quot;Il JSON non è valido.&quot;,
      &quot;error&quot;
    );

    return;

  }


  const blob =
    new Blob(
      [
        JSON.stringify(
          data,
          null,
          2
        )
      ],
      {
        type:
          &quot;application/json;charset=utf-8&quot;
      }
    );


  const url =
    URL.createObjectURL(blob);


  const a =
    document.createElement(&quot;a&quot;);


  const title =
    data.title ||
    &quot;ADEC_theory&quot;;


  a.href =
    url;

  a.download =
    `${title.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi, &quot;_&quot;)}.json`;


  document.body.appendChild(a);

  a.click();

  a.remove();


  URL.revokeObjectURL(
    url
  );


  showToast(
    &quot;JSON esportato.&quot;,
    &quot;success&quot;
  );

}


/* =========================================================
   VALIDATION HISTORY (v0.8.1)
   ========================================================= */

function exportValidationHistory() {

  if (!validationHistory.length) {

    showToast(
      &quot;Nessuna validazione in questa sessione da esportare.&quot;,
      &quot;warning&quot;
    );

    return;

  }

  const payload = {
    adecHistoryFormat: 1,
    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    entries: validationHistory
  };

  const blob =
    new Blob(
      [ JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) ],
      { type: &quot;application/json;charset=utf-8&quot; }
    );

  const url =
    URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const a =
    document.createElement(&quot;a&quot;);

  a.href = url;
  a.download = &quot;ADEC_validation_history.json&quot;;

  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

  showToast(
    `Storico esportato (${validationHistory.length} voci).`,
    &quot;success&quot;
  );

}


function importValidationHistory(file) {

  const reader = new FileReader();

  reader.onload = () =&gt; {

    let payload;

    try {
      payload = JSON.parse(reader.result);
    } catch (error) {
      showToast(&quot;Il file di storico non è un JSON valido.&quot;, &quot;error&quot;);
      return;
    }

    const entries =
      Array.isArray(payload?.entries)
        ? payload.entries
        : Array.isArray(payload)
          ? payload
          : null;

    if (!entries) {
      showToast(&#39;Formato non riconosciuto: atteso un campo &quot;entries&quot;.&#39;, &quot;error&quot;);
      return;
    }

    /*
     * Merge conservativo: le voci importate vengono anteposte a quelle
     * della sessione corrente (ordine cronologico), poi viene applicato
     * lo stesso limite usato per le validazioni correnti. Nessun campo
     * viene reinterpretato: sono già riepiloghi, non risultati completi.
     */
    validationHistory = [...entries, ...validationHistory];

    if (validationHistory.length &gt; VALIDATION_HISTORY_LIMIT) {
      validationHistory =
        validationHistory.slice(
          validationHistory.length - VALIDATION_HISTORY_LIMIT
        );
    }

    showToast(
      `Storico importato: ${entries.length} voci aggiunte.`,
      &quot;success&quot;
    );

  };

  reader.onerror = () =&gt; {
    showToast(&quot;Impossibile leggere il file di storico.&quot;, &quot;error&quot;);
  };

  reader.readAsText(file);

}


/* =========================================================
   FILE UPLOAD
   ========================================================= */

$(&quot;uploadButton&quot;)
  .addEventListener(
    &quot;click&quot;,
    () =&gt; {

      $(&quot;fileInput&quot;).click();

    }
  );


$(&quot;fileInput&quot;)
  .addEventListener(
    &quot;change&quot;,
    event =&gt; {

      const file =
        event.target.files[0];

      if (!file)
        return;


      const reader =
        new FileReader();


      reader.onload =
        () =&gt; {

          $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)
            .value =
            reader.result;


          $(&quot;editorState&quot;)
            .classList.remove(&quot;hidden&quot;);


          updateNodeHint();

          runValidation();

        };


      reader.onerror =
        () =&gt; {

          showToast(
            &quot;Impossibile leggere il file.&quot;,
            &quot;error&quot;
          );

        };


      reader.readAsText(
        file
      );

    }
  );


/* =========================================================
   DIAGNOSTIC LOG / DETAILS EXPORT
   ========================================================= */

function safeDownloadName(value, fallback) {
  const base = String(value || fallback || &quot;ADEC&quot;)
    .normalize(&quot;NFKD&quot;)
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi, &quot;_&quot;)
    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, &quot;&quot;)
    .slice(0, 120);
  return base || fallback || &quot;ADEC&quot;;
}

function downloadTextFile(filename, text, mime = &quot;text/plain;charset=utf-8&quot;) {
  const blob = new Blob([text], { type: mime });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement(&quot;a&quot;);
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() =&gt; URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 0);
}

function downloadDiagnosticLog() {
  const log = $(&quot;diagnosticLog&quot;);
  if (!log || !log.innerText.trim()) {
    showToast(&quot;Nessun Diagnostic Log da salvare.&quot;, &quot;warning&quot;);
    return;
  }

  const title = currentResult?.title || &quot;ADEC_theory&quot;;
  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, &quot;-&quot;);
  const filename = `${safeDownloadName(title, &quot;ADEC_theory&quot;)}_Diagnostic_Log_${stamp}.txt`;
  downloadTextFile(filename, log.innerText.trim() + &quot;\n&quot;, &quot;text/plain;charset=utf-8&quot;);
  showToast(&quot;Diagnostic Log salvato.&quot;, &quot;success&quot;);
}

function downloadValidationDetails() {
  if (!currentResult) {
    showToast(&quot;Nessun risultato di validazione da salvare.&quot;, &quot;warning&quot;);
    return;
  }

  const currentText = $(&quot;jsonEditor&quot;)?.value.trim() || &quot;&quot;;
  if (validatedEditorText === null || currentText !== validatedEditorText) {
    showToast(&quot;Il JSON è stato modificato. Esegui nuovamente la validazione.&quot;, &quot;warning&quot;);
    return;
  }

  renderValidationDetails();
  const body = $(&quot;detailModalBody&quot;);
  const subtitle = $(&quot;detailModalSubtitle&quot;)?.textContent || &quot;ADEC Validation Details&quot;;
  const title = currentResult.title || &quot;ADEC_theory&quot;;
  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, &quot;-&quot;);
  const filename = `${safeDownloadName(title, &quot;ADEC_theory&quot;)}_Validation_Details_${stamp}.html`;

  const html = `&lt;!doctype html&gt;
&lt;html lang=&quot;it&quot;&gt;
&lt;head&gt;
&lt;meta charset=&quot;utf-8&quot;&gt;
&lt;meta name=&quot;viewport&quot; content=&quot;width=device-width,initial-scale=1&quot;&gt;
&lt;title&gt;${escapeHtml(title)} — ADEC Validation Details&lt;/title&gt;
&lt;style&gt;
body{margin:0;background:#020617;color:#cbd5e1;font-family:Inter,system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,sans-serif;line-height:1.5;padding:32px}h1{font-size:20px;color:#fff;margin:0 0 4px}h2,h3{color:#e2e8f0}p{color:#94a3b8}strong{color:#e2e8f0}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}td,th{border:1px solid #334155;padding:8px;text-align:left;font-size:12px}.rounded-xl,.rounded-lg{border-radius:12px}.border{border:1px solid #1e293b}.bg-slate-950,.bg-slate-900{background:#0f172a}.p-4,.p-3{padding:16px}.text-slate-300{color:#cbd5e1}.text-slate-400,.text-slate-500{color:#94a3b8}.text-emerald-400{color:#34d399}.text-amber-400{color:#fbbf24}.text-red-400{color:#f87171}.text-blue-400{color:#60a5fa}.text-violet-300{color:#c4b5fd}.text-cyan-400{color:#22d3ee}.text-fuchsia-400{color:#e879f9}.mt-1{margin-top:4px}.mt-2{margin-top:8px}.mt-3{margin-top:12px}.mt-4{margin-top:16px}.mb-4{margin-bottom:16px}.grid{display:grid;gap:12px}.lg\:grid-cols-2,.sm\:grid-cols-2{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}@media(max-width:800px){.lg\:grid-cols-2,.sm\:grid-cols-2{grid-template-columns:1fr}}ul{padding-left:20px}li{margin:4px 0}.overflow-x-auto{overflow-x:auto}&lt;/style&gt;
&lt;/head&gt;
&lt;body&gt;
&lt;h1&gt;Dettagli validazione ADEC&lt;/h1&gt;
&lt;p&gt;${escapeHtml(subtitle)}&lt;/p&gt;
${body?.innerHTML || &quot;&quot;}
&lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;`;

  downloadTextFile(filename, html, &quot;text/html;charset=utf-8&quot;);
  showToast(&quot;Dettagli della validazione salvati.&quot;, &quot;success&quot;);
}

/*
 * The detail modal is declared after this script block.
 * Use delegated events so the handlers remain valid regardless
 * of DOM declaration order.
 */
document.addEventListener(
  &quot;click&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    if (
      event.target.closest(&quot;#saveDetailsButton&quot;)
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      downloadValidationDetails();
      return;

    }

    if (
      event.target.closest(&quot;#closeDetailModal&quot;)
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();

      closeValidationDetails();

      return;

    }

    if (
      event.target === $(&quot;detailModal&quot;)
    ) {

      closeValidationDetails();

    }

  }
);

document.addEventListener(
  &quot;keydown&quot;,
  event =&gt; {

    if (event.key === &quot;Escape&quot;) {

      const modal = $(&quot;detailModal&quot;);

      if (
        modal &amp;&amp;
        !modal.classList.contains(&quot;hidden&quot;)
      ) {

        closeValidationDetails();

      }

    }

  }
);

/* =========================================================
   INITIALIZATION
   ========================================================= */

function init() {

  // Nessuna teoria viene caricata automaticamente.
  // Il motore parte vuoto e analizza esclusivamente
  // il JSON fornito dall&#39;utente.

  // FIX: lucide viene caricato da un CDN esterno (unpkg.com/lucide,
  // vedi &lt;script&gt; in &lt;head&gt;). Se la rete è assente/bloccata, il CDN
  // non risponde o viene bloccato da un ad-blocker, l&#39;oggetto globale
  // &quot;lucide&quot; non esiste e la chiamata sotto lanciava
  // &quot;ReferenceError: lucide is not defined&quot;, interrompendo init().
  // Ora l&#39;assenza della libreria viene gestita in modo controllato:
  // le icone semplicemente non vengono renderizzate, ma il resto
  // dell&#39;applicazione (validazione, editor, grafo) resta funzionante.
  if (typeof lucide !== &quot;undefined&quot; &amp;&amp; lucide.createIcons) {
    lucide.createIcons();
  } else {
    console.warn(
      &quot;ADEC: libreria &#39;lucide&#39; non disponibile (CDN non raggiungibile). &quot; +
      &quot;Le icone non verranno renderizzate, il resto dell&#39;app funziona normalmente.&quot;
    );
  }

}


init();

&lt;/script&gt;


&lt;!-- =========================================================
     DETAIL MODAL
     ========================================================= --&gt;
&lt;div id=&quot;detailModal&quot; class=&quot;fixed inset-0 z-[100] hidden items-center justify-center bg-black/70 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm&quot;&gt;
  &lt;div class=&quot;flex max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-5xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl&quot;&gt;
    &lt;div class=&quot;flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 px-5 py-4&quot;&gt;
      &lt;div&gt;
        &lt;div class=&quot;text-sm font-semibold text-white&quot;&gt;Dettagli validazione ADEC&lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;div id=&quot;detailModalSubtitle&quot; class=&quot;mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500&quot;&gt;Analisi dettagliata della teoria&lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div class=&quot;flex items-center gap-2&quot;&gt;
        &lt;button id=&quot;saveDetailsButton&quot; type=&quot;button&quot; title=&quot;Salva i dettagli completi della validazione&quot; class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-500/20&quot;&gt;Salva dettagli&lt;/button&gt;
        &lt;button id=&quot;closeDetailModal&quot; type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700&quot;&gt;Chiudi&lt;/button&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;
    &lt;div id=&quot;detailModalBody&quot; class=&quot;overflow-y-auto p-5&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
  &lt;/div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;


&lt;div id=&quot;validationScopeNote&quot; class=&quot;mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400 text-center&quot;&gt;
    ADEC valida la rappresentazione strutturale, semantica, formale, matematica e algebrica dichiarata.
  La validazione non equivale alla dimostrazione della teoria e non sostituisce un proof checker esterno.
&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
/* ADEC v0.8.3 — robust Details window close handling
   FIX: i selettori puntavano a ID inesistenti nel markup
   (#detailsModal, #closeDetails, ecc. — con la &quot;s&quot; di troppo e
   suffissi mai usati). Il modale reale è #detailModal con
   pulsante di chiusura #closeDetailModal: questo blocco era
   quindi codice morto (querySelectorAll restituiva sempre liste
   vuote). Ora i selettori coincidono col markup effettivo e la
   funzione fa davvero da rete di sicurezza ridondante rispetto
   alla chiusura già gestita nello script principale. */
(function () {
  function closeDetails() {
    const candidates = [
      &quot;#detailModal&quot;,
      &quot;[data-details-modal]&quot;,
      &quot;[role=&#39;dialog&#39;][data-details]&quot;
    ];

    let closed = false;

    candidates.forEach(selector =&gt; {
      document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el =&gt; {
        el.classList.add(&quot;hidden&quot;);
        el.classList.remove(&quot;flex&quot;);
        el.setAttribute(&quot;aria-hidden&quot;, &quot;true&quot;);
        closed = true;
      });
    });

    // Restore page scrolling if the modal had locked it.
    document.body.classList.remove(&quot;overflow-hidden&quot;);
    document.documentElement.classList.remove(&quot;overflow-hidden&quot;);

    return closed;
  }

  document.addEventListener(&quot;click&quot;, function (event) {
    const closeButton = event.target.closest(
      &quot;#closeDetailModal, [data-close-details], [data-close=&#39;details&#39;]&quot;
    );

    if (closeButton) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      closeDetails();
      return;
    }

    // Close when clicking the backdrop itself.
    const modal = event.target.closest(
      &quot;#detailModal, [data-details-modal], [role=&#39;dialog&#39;][data-details]&quot;
    );

    if (modal &amp;&amp; event.target === modal) {
      closeDetails();
    }
  });

  document.addEventListener(&quot;keydown&quot;, function (event) {
    if (event.key === &quot;Escape&quot;) {
      closeDetails();
    }
  });
})();
&lt;/script&gt;








&lt;script&gt;
/* ADEC v0.8.1 — explicit loaded theory name */
(function () {
  function updateLoadedTheoryName(result) {
    const box = document.getElementById(&quot;loadedTheoryName&quot;);
    const value = document.getElementById(&quot;loadedTheoryNameValue&quot;);
    if (!box || !value) return;

    const name =
      result &amp;&amp;
      typeof result.title === &quot;string&quot; &amp;&amp;
      result.title.trim()
        ? result.title.trim()
        : &quot;Nessuna teoria caricata&quot;;

    value.textContent = name;
    box.style.display = result &amp;&amp; result.title ? &quot;&quot; : &quot;none&quot;;
  }

  window.updateAdecLoadedTheoryName = updateLoadedTheoryName;

  // FIX: il tentativo di monkey-patch di window.setCurrentResult è stato
  // rimosso — quella funzione non è mai stata definita da nessuna parte
  // nel file (currentResult è un binding lessicale interno allo script
  // principale, non una proprietà di window), quindi il blocco
  // &quot;if (typeof originalSet === &#39;function&#39;)&quot; era sempre falso: codice
  // morto che non veniva mai eseguito. L&#39;unica sincronizzazione che
  // funziona davvero è quella guidata dall&#39;evento, qui sotto.
  document.addEventListener(&quot;ADEC:result-loaded&quot;, event =&gt; {
    updateLoadedTheoryName(event.detail);
  });

  // currentResult è un binding lessicale, non window.currentResult.
  // La sincronizzazione è quindi guidata dall&#39;evento ADEC:result-loaded,
  // disparato da runValidation() nello script principale.
})();
&lt;/script&gt;


&lt;script&gt;
/* Keep theory name synchronized in diagnostic/details headers. */
(function () {
  function syncTheoryName() {
    const result = arguments.length &amp;&amp; arguments[0]?.detail
      ? arguments[0].detail
      : null;
    const title = result?.title
      ? String(result.title).trim()
      : &quot;&quot;;

    if (!title) return;

    document.querySelectorAll(
      &quot;#detailModalSubtitle, #diagnosticTheoryName, [data-theory-name]&quot;
    ).forEach(el =&gt; {
      el.textContent = title;
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener(&quot;ADEC:result-loaded&quot;, syncTheoryName);
})();
&lt;/script&gt;
